## Mobile Field Navigation Milestone

- Mobile Field Navigation now uses one permission-aware field dock for browsing pages: `หน้าหลัก`, `ทรัพย์สิน`, `สแกน`, `ตรวจนับ`, and `เพิ่มเติม`. Dashboard, Asset, Scan, and Audit destinations reuse existing RBAC checks; an employee without broad `asset:view` falls back to My Assets, and `เพิ่มเติม` opens the existing permission-filtered Sidebar instead of creating a second menu tree.
- The dashboard shell has mutually exclusive Navigation and Focus Task modes. General Asset Scan, Audit Scan, asset create/edit/detail, asset transactions, audit round create/detail, and maintenance/disposal detail routes hide the field dock so existing scanner, save, and `MobileActionBar` controls own the bottom edge. Audit Pending remains in Navigation Mode because `Mark Not Found` belongs to the pending queue rather than the successful scan result.
- Field navigation adds safe-area content reservation only while visible and hides when `visualViewport` reports a mobile keyboard. Navigation destinations have visible labels, Lucide icons, `aria-current`, focus rings, and touch targets of at least 44px. The topbar mobile menu and scan shortcut are not duplicated while the field dock is available; Focus Task Mode keeps the normal mobile menu escape.
- Controller browser QA passed at 375x812, 390x844, 768x1024, and 1024x800. Asset Register showed exactly one visible field dock below the `lg` shell breakpoint, no Audit action bar, no body overflow, and target heights from 47.75px to 63.75px. At 1024px the field dock was hidden and the 256px desktop Sidebar was visible, preventing the prior 768-1023px navigation gap. Audit Scan showed no field dock; after selecting an in-round item it showed exactly one existing Audit action bar with 56px/48px/48px actions and no body overflow. Manual Asset Tag/Serial/QR entry remained visible before selection. Navigation Mode is independent from transient dock visibility, so opening the More drawer or mobile keyboard does not reintroduce the hamburger or shift the Topbar. More exposes the existing Sidebar through `aria-expanded` and `aria-controls`; it moves focus to the drawer's close button when opened, restores focus to the More trigger after close-button/overlay/Escape dismissal, and deliberately leaves focus with the next page after a drawer link is chosen. Regression coverage guards that focus contract. Latest verification passed the 14 focused mobile-navigation tests, `npm test` 677/677, scoped ESLint, Prisma generation, Next.js 16.2.4 TypeScript/build, and 54/54 static pages. Real-device keyboard, rotation, safe-area, and screen-reader focus behavior still require Android/iPhone UAT.

## Current High-Level Modules

- Dashboard and Work Center
- Asset Register and Asset Detail
- Asset Clone, Batch Create, Import/Export, and Data Quality tools
- Scan/Search Asset and QR Label printing
- Check-out, Check-in, Transfer, and custody timeline
- Audit rounds, scan workflow, findings review, and close-round controls
- Repair records (one form) and Preventive Maintenance reminder plans
- Disposal request, approval, evidence, and execution lifecycle
- Disposal production-readiness separates the paginated queue from `/disposal/new`, adds stage-aware detail ownership/next actions, enforces lifecycle/RBAC/SOD and active employee checks in APIs, validates decisions/execution by disposal type, and adds an auditable 2-100 item packet at `/disposal/batch/new`. Readiness now blocks open checkout, active maintenance, operational audit/finding, installed component, and license-assignment conflicts while keeping blocked search results visible with reasons. `/disposal/batches` and `/disposal/batches/{id}` provide batch history/result workspaces; shared evidence is stored once on the batch and inherited by children, while approval and execution remain independent per child. The queue shows exact ranges, validates date order, exposes batch source links, keeps desktop actions sticky, and progressively reveals mobile details. Mobile request detail promotes review/execute to the primary bottom action and keeps asset/print/history secondary. Single and batch creation use compact numbered sections and a server-bounded eligible-asset search, conditionally claim asset status to prevent concurrent duplicate requests, and retry bounded document-number unique conflicts. The batch schema migration `2026-07-13-add-disposal-batches.sql` is already recorded as applied for `asset_management`; a different target database must apply it before deployment. Keep `workflow_approval_min_approvers=1` until disposal has a multi-approval record model.
- Disposal Bulk Approval is an approver-only queue efficiency layer at `/disposal`, not a second lifecycle: users need `disposal:approve`, SOD still blocks requester/creator self-approval, and the API independently checks authentication, permission, active/pending state, asset lifecycle, and policy. A selection contains only currently loaded eligible queue rows, never spans pages, and is limited to 50. The server preview groups eligible and blocked rows without mutation; it is advisory, and commit re-reads/revalidates every request. Each successful request commits independently with its own approver/timestamp, movement, audit record, and affected parent-batch status, so a concurrency change or processing failure produces an item-level blocked/failed partial result without rolling back other approvals or duplicating records. One optional shared approval remark is copied only to successful requests and does not overwrite their sale/salvage values, evidence, type, or other request-specific data. Bulk reject remains intentionally unavailable. Desktop uses queue-table selection and a contextual toolbar; mobile uses an explicit selection mode in the list flow, not an extra fixed bottom bar, so it does not collide with Mobile Field Navigation.
- Disposal Bulk Execution is a separate `disposal:edit` efficiency layer available only in the approved/awaiting-execution queue. Operators explicitly enter selection mode and may choose up to 20 currently loaded requests of one disposal type. Preview and commit use the same locked shared execution date, executor, final Disposed/Retired status, optional shared recipient, and optional historical-exception values. For `sell`, `donate`, and `dispose`, the shared recipient is a fallback only: it supplies the effective execution recipient when the authoritative request recipient is blank and never overwrites a nonblank request value. Every Preview/Commit item includes `recipientName` and `recipientSource` (`request`, `shared`, or `null`) so the effective value is reviewable before Commit. Each request otherwise keeps its own document, sale/salvage values, execution detail, evidence, movement, and audit history. Commit revalidates each request in an independent serializable transaction, preserves preview-blocked rows in the final result, supports partial success, and retries only stable deduplicated unresolved IDs. The historical no-evidence exception is shown only to `system_admin` when every selected request has zero active item and inherited batch evidence, with the existing 20-2,000 character reason and acknowledgement. No schema or migration is added by bulk execution. Manual acceptance evidence for this fallback must use Preview only: select two approved donations with blank recipients, enter `ปลายทางทดสอบสำหรับ Preview`, verify both show `ใช้ค่าร่วม`, and close without permanent Commit. Full verification and authenticated browser UAT remain controller-owned; this handoff does not mark either as passed.
- Reports, accounting/depreciation views, and exports
- Master Data for organization, locations, categories, brands/models, employees, suppliers
- Admin settings, RBAC, system logs, readiness, storage governance, scheduler settings, LDAP/AD

## Handover Mode Change (2026-08-31)

- New Check-outs require `handoverMode`: `permanent_assignment` changes the asset from `Ready` to `In Use`; `temporary_loan` changes it to `Checked Out`.
- Permanent assignment is employee-only, requires a custodian, and has no expected-return date. Temporary loan requires an expected-return date on or after the handover date.
- Check-in validates the active Checkout and its authoritative source status inside the transaction. Permanent assignments return from `In Use`; temporary loans return from `Checked Out`.
- Active legacy rows with no mode are disabled on Check-in and surfaced by Asset State Review; completed legacy history remains readable as unspecified and is never inferred from a null due date.
- `prisma/manual-migrations/2026-08-31-add-checkout-handover-mode.sql` was applied to `asset_management` on 2026-08-31 after the operator confirmed a fresh backup. The accepted checksum prefix is `9063246fff57`.
- The migration backfilled only `GRL-COM-06-0001 / HO-202606-0002` and `SNI-EQU-19-0336 / HO-202608-0003`. Both are active `permanent_assignment` Checkouts in `In Use`, each with one migration movement and one System Log entry; no active null-mode Checkout remained after verification.
- The first apply attempt (`37874f3a6a43`) failed before any change because SQL Server compiled references to the newly added column in the same batch. The transaction rolled back. The accepted migration defers those statements with `sp_executesql`, and the migration runner now preserves nested SQL Server error details instead of recording an empty AggregateError message.

## Development Database (2026-10-07)

- `asset_management_dev` was restored on the same SQL Server instance from a COPY_ONLY backup of Production taken 2026-10-07 (`MSSQL17.ALPHA\MSSQL\Backup\asset_management_copyonly_20261007.bak` on the server), switched to SIMPLE recovery, and its log shrunk to about 264 MB.
- Login `asset_dev` is `db_owner` on `asset_management_dev` only; it is denied on `asset_management` and on other company databases on the instance.
- In the dev copy `ldap_sync_enabled=false` and `ldap_sync_mode=preview`; LDAP login still works. The `manual_migration_history` rows were re-keyed to `databaseName = asset_management_dev` because the ledger filters by database name; without that every migration shows as pending.
- Attachments in the dev copy point at Production absolute paths and return 500 locally (see finding E4).

## shadcn/ui Foundation (2026-10-07)

- UI primitives are shadcn/ui (style `new-york`, Tailwind 4, package `radix-ui`) in `src/components/ui/`. Add more by copying the component source from the shadcn registry (`new-york-v4`) by hand: rewrite the `cn` import to `@/lib/utils` and `@/registry/new-york-v4/ui/*` imports to `@/components/ui/*`, remove any `dark:` classes, and give touch targets `min-h-11` on mobile. Do not use `npx shadcn@latest add`: CLI 4.21 emits `import { cn } from "cn"`, adds an unrelated npm package `cn`, and may rewrite `src/app/globals.css`.
- Tokens live in `:root` of `src/app/globals.css` with shadcn names plus five values per status tone (`{tone}`, `-foreground`, `-soft`, `-border`, `-hover`). `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` fails the build if a pair drops below 4.5:1. Use `bg-{tone}-soft` and `hover:bg-{tone}-hover`; opacity tints are blocked by `tests/ui-overlay-guards.test.ts`.
- Fonts (since UI round 4, 2026-10-10; Thai face changed 2026-10-16): IBM Plex Sans (Latin, tabular digits) before Noto Sans Thai Looped (Thai, `--font-thai`), plus IBM Plex Mono for codes, through `next/font/google` in `src/app/layout.tsx` only; `font-sans` / `font-mono` resolve to them. The loopless IBM Plex Sans Thai was replaced because it was hard to read at 12-14px. PDFs keep the embedded Noto Sans Thai.
- `Button`, `Badge` and field variants are in `button-variants.ts`, `badge-variants.ts` and `field-variants.ts` so `node --test` can import them.
- Confirmation: `useConfirm()` from `src/components/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx` (provider mounted in `DashboardShell`). Leave-page guards use `shouldGuardLinkClick` from `src/lib/navigation-guard.ts`.
- Focus return: dialogs opened from a menu item or other non-Trigger control would otherwise lose focus (Radix sends it to a missing Trigger). `AccessibleDialog` takes `returnFocusRef` and an optional `fallbackFocusRef` (used when the opener has left the DOM); `confirm({ …, returnFocusRef })` does the same for confirmations.
- Rules: menus never contain dialogs; busy dialogs pass `busy`; database status colors are no longer shown on badges (since UI round 4 the marker uses the tone color and `StatusBadge` has no `color` prop). Design spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-ui-shadcn-foundation-design.md`.

## Asset Register Redesign (2026-10-07)

- `/[locale]/assets` is split into single-purpose components in `src/components/assets/asset-register-*.tsx`: toolbar, search field, filter sheet, status tabs, filter chips, row actions, sort/column/export menus, mobile list, and the table orchestrator. Pure view-model helpers (`asset-register-filters`, `-status-tabs`, `-chips`, `-sort`, `-columns`) live in `src/lib` and are unit-tested. Since phase 2B plan 3 they sit on the shared primitives: the status tabs wrap `TabNav`, the chips `FilterChipList` and the row actions `RowActions` (ช1; `AssetRegisterRowActions` builds its data-only props); the toolbar and the filter sheet use `FilterSelect`, the search field `SearchField` and the menu triggers `Button`; the table uses `DataTableBody`, `ClickableTableRow`, `DataTableHead sort`, `TableEmptyRow` and `DataTableFoot` with its own dense `headerClasses` / `cellClasses` (spec 2B I3, I5–I7, I9).
- Filter state stays in the existing URL params (`src/lib/asset-list-query.ts`). `AssetRegisterNavigationProvider` applies changes with `router.replace(…, { scroll: false })` inside `startTransition`, shows them at once through `useOptimistic`, and merges changes made inside one debounce window through a ref. Status tabs and chips are plain links.
- Status counts come from one `prisma.asset.groupBy({ by: ["statusId"] })` over `buildAssetStatusCountWhere(filters)`, the list's own where clause without `statusId`. The cross-scope filter runs once and serves both the counts and the list.
- Thumbnails: `GET /api/attachments/[id]/thumbnail` returns a `sharp` 96px WebP with `Cache-Control: private, max-age=604800, immutable` and `ETag "{id}-96"`. It answers 304 after the permission check, 415 for non-images, 404 for a missing row or file, and 422 when an image can't be read. The view rule shared with the full-file route lives in `src/lib/attachment-access.ts` (`assertCanViewAttachment`). `sharp` is now a direct dependency; check that the Production `npm ci` log installs `@img/sharp-linux-x64`.
- `MasterDataHeader` takes `compactOnMobile` (title and create button share one row, subtitle hidden below `md`); only the register uses it so far. Since phase 2B plan 1 the prop lives on the shared `PageHeader`; since plan 2B-3 the register calls `PageHeader compactOnMobile` itself (still the prop's only user), and `MasterDataHeader`, a thin wrapper of `PageHeader`, had no caller left, and plan 2B-5 deleted it with `ColumnHeader`, `SortableColumnHeader` and `MasterDataPagination`.
- Design spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-asset-register-redesign-design.md`. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-asset-register-redesign.md`.

## Audit Scan Redesign (2026-10-07)

- `/[locale]/audit/rounds/[id]/scan` renders `AuditScanWorkspace` (`src/components/audit/audit-scan-workspace.tsx`). The 2,065-line `audit-scan-form.tsx` is gone; the screen is split into header, room picker, room list, search, lookup card, camera, check form/field/panel, missing-component dialog, saved banner and offline bar. `audit-scan-panels.tsx` keeps only `AuditComponentPanel` and `AuditQrScannerOverlay`.
- All rules are pure and unit-tested in `src/lib/audit-scan-session.ts`: room list and room options, progress, in-round search (exact → prefix → contains, pending first, max 10), check defaults (new check → expected values with location = selected room; edit → saved actual values, never the room; out of scope → register values with location = room), mismatch diff mirroring the scan route (location skipped for licences, custodian only for `personal`), photo rule, department suggestion, `applyScanResult`, `mergeStatusUpdates`.
- **Photo rule (user decision 2026-10-07):** an in-round save needs a photo only when the condition differs; out-of-scope keeps the old rule (any difference). Client-side only; the server rule is unchanged.
- Data: the page loads slim rows with `loadAuditScanRows` (`src/lib/audit-scan-data.ts` + pure mapper `src/lib/audit-scan-rows.ts`) — no per-row component includes; component counts come from one `groupBy` filtered by relation (never an id list, so no 2,100-parameter risk). Components load lazily through `scan-lookup` when a sheet opens.
- Sharing progress: `GET /api/audit-rounds/[id]/scan-status?since=<ISO>` (`audit:edit`) returns `{ serverTime, roundStatus, items }` for rows with `updatedAt > since − 10 s`. `serverTime` is taken before the query; the 10 s overlap catches Serializable scan transactions that commit after their `updatedAt` stamp; the client merges by `itemId`. The client polls every 30 s while visible, on tab return (at most once per 5 s), on room change and after each save.
- Scan route changes: re-saving closes pending findings whose mismatch no longer applies (`reviewStatus: "rejected"`, remark "ยกเลิกเพราะแก้ผลตรวจ"; `not_found` and component findings untouched), refreshes `actualValue` on findings that still apply, and returns `scannedByName`. `scan-lookup` returns `{ status: "candidates", matches }` (≤5, `contains` on tag/serial/fixed-asset code) when the exact lookup misses and the input has ≥3 characters. The pending page accepts `?locationId=` and shows/keeps the room filter.
- Offline: `upsertQueuedAuditScanAsync` keeps one queued save per asset (newer result wins, earlier photos are kept); the queue sends itself on `online` and after each poll; failed entries stay with their error until sent manually or removed.
- Staff guide: `docs/18_AUDIT_SCAN_GUIDE_TH.md`. Design spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-07-audit-scan-redesign-design.md`. Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-07-audit-scan-redesign.md`. No migration and no new dependency.

## Dependency Security Update (2026-10-08)

- `next` 16.4.0 (exact, with `eslint-config-next` 16.4.0), `next-auth` ^5.0.0-beta.32 (`@auth/core` 0.41.3), `sharp` ^0.35.5; `npm audit fix` without `--force` also moved Prisma 7.8.0 → 7.10.0 inside `^7.8.0`. No app code changed.
- Always regenerate the lockfile with npm 10 (`npx -y npm@10.9.4 install …`): Production runs npm 10, and an npm-11 lockfile broke `npm ci` there before.
- Ignore npm audit's suggested "fix" `prisma@6.x` — it is a downgrade. The remaining high findings (`prisma`, `@prisma/config`, `deepmerge-ts`, `mysql2`) come from the Prisma CLI and wait for a Prisma 7.x release.
- `next dev` 16.4 writes and maintains the `BEGIN/END:nextjs-agent-rules` block at the top of `AGENTS.md`; commit its changes rather than reverting them (or set `agentRules: false` in `next.config.ts` to stop it).

## Thai Copy Cleanup (2026-10-08)

- Glossary: `docs/19_THAI_GLOSSARY.md`. `tests/thai-glossary.test.ts` checks every namespace of `messages/th.json` (`enforcedNamespaces = Object.keys(th)`); exceptions need a reason in `keyExceptions`. `tests/messages-parity.test.ts` keeps th/en keys and `{placeholders}` identical. Edit messages only with `node scripts/messages-edit.mjs <changes.json>` (`{ "set": { "th": {…}, "en": {…} }, "delete": [...] }`, dotted keys).
- 319 unused keys were deleted (`scripts/find-unused-messages.mjs` lists candidates). Namespaces built at runtime stay whole: `systemLogPage` (`src/lib/system-log-presenter.ts`) and `notifications.*Detail`.
- Server errors: `src/lib/api-error-catalog.ts` maps known English server texts to `apiErrors.*` keys. In screens use `useApiError()` (`{ describe, toast }`) or `<ApiErrorText error/>`: Thai first, the original text as a small second line, Thai server text passes through. `tests/api-error-catalog.test.ts` fails when an API throws a text with no catalog entry; `tests/api-error-ui.test.ts` fails when a `.ts/.tsx` screen toasts raw server text.
- Unexpected errors (review S9): `errorResponse` (`src/lib/api-response.ts`) sends a message only when `isExposableError` (`src/lib/api-error-exposure.ts`) allows it. Hidden: non-`Error` values, built-in `TypeError`/`RangeError`/…, `PrismaClient*`, `DriverAdapterError`, errors with `syscall`/`errno`. Hidden errors return 500 `Unexpected error · ref <8 hex>` and `console.error("[api error] ref …", error)` logs the same ref. Throw user-facing messages as plain `Error`. The companies routes, LDAP sync, notification digest, transaction cancellation and the integration API (`[integration api error] requestId …`) follow the same rule.
- Years: Thai pages show พ.ศ. through `toDisplayYear` / `fromDisplayYear` / `displayYearRange` (`src/lib/display-year.ts`). The new-round form takes 2543–2643 and sends Gregorian. `<html lang>` follows the locale.
- Status codes on screens go through labels: `src/lib/audit-item-status-labels.ts` (audit item `auditStatus` / `reconcileStatus`, namespace `auditFinding`) plus `getAuditRoundItemStatusLabelKey` / `getAuditRoundItemResultLabelKey`. Unknown codes show raw. The import preview shows Thai field names from `asset.importField.*`. Excel import accepts both old headers (พื้นที่ …) and new ones (ที่ตั้ง …).
- Data: `prisma/manual-migrations/2026-10-08-thai-status-wording.sql` renames status `Checked Out` to ถูกยืม and rewords 4 descriptions with guarded `UPDATE`s (only rows still holding the old default text). Applied on `asset_management_dev` 2026-10-08. **Production needs a backup and the user's approval with the full SQL first.** `prisma/seed.ts` matches.
- Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-thai-copy-cleanup-design.md` · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-thai-copy-cleanup.md`. Still English on Thai pages (deferred): system-log/approval-history field names humanised from code (`humanizeKey`), readiness detected values ("configured", "needs review"), a few screen-reader labels in the top bar, Zod validation details as the error second line.

## UI Round 4 Phase 1 Foundation (2026-10-10)

- Branch `feat/ui-foundation` (from `master` `60bea4f`). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-10-ui-foundation-design.md` (§15 overrides earlier sections) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-10-ui-foundation.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`. Phase 1 of 4: tokens and shared helpers only, so every page changes without page-by-page edits. No API, schema, RBAC, migration or dependency change.
- Colors ("light shell", from the app icon): the token names are unchanged, only the values. `--primary` #1E4F94, `--brand-navy` #083161, `--brand-accent` #18A0A8, `--info` is teal #0A6E75 (no longer equal to primary), `--input` #7C8BA0 (3:1 field borders), `--ring` #10858D. New tokens `--canvas` (shell background), `--primary-border`, `--sidebar-active-foreground`, `--sidebar-active-icon`, `--sidebar-border`; the sidebar tokens are now light. `bg-scrim` and `shadow-overlay` live in the plain `@theme` block. The first `:root {` in `globals.css` must stay the token block (`readRootTokens` reads only it) and every token needs a `--color-*` mapping in `@theme inline`.
- Focus: one ring color. A codemod changed `(focus|focus-visible|focus-within):(ring|border)-(primary|brand-accent)` to `-ring`; a base `:focus-visible` outline covers elements without a ring, and an unlayered `forced-colors` rule draws a `CanvasText` outline.
- Fonts: IBM Plex Sans + IBM Plex Sans Thai + IBM Plex Mono, loaded only in `src/app/layout.tsx`. `text-sm` line height is 22/14 everywhere; Thai pages raise xs/xl/2xl/3xl (ratios, in `@layer base`). New utilities `num` (tabular digits, on MetricCard values; tables get tabular digits from a base rule) and `tag` (Plex Mono, not used yet). Toasts use the app font.
- Surfaces: panels, metric cards, the topbar, the register toolbar and the mobile bottom bars are flat; dialogs, sheets, popovers and menus use `shadow-overlay` over `bg-scrim`; sheets animate 250/200ms; with reduced motion, dialogs, alert dialogs and sheets (and their scrims) stop animating, while dropdowns and popovers still fade and zoom. Base rules: default border color, `color-scheme: only light`, `accent-color`, `::selection`, thin scrollbars. Light only: no `.dark`, no `dark:`.
- `StatusBadge`: two tiers (calm: ring/dot/bar marker with muted text; attention: warning triangle and danger diamond with soft fill and border). The `color` prop and `getStatusDotColor` were removed, so database status colors are no longer shown. Tone mapping is unchanged.
- Shell: white sidebar with a brand mark (app icon + `nav.brandName`) and three sections (`nav.sectionDaily` / `nav.sectionAssets` / `nav.sectionSystem`, filtered by `filterNavigationSectionsByPermission`), a solid navy active row with a teal icon and `aria-current`, chosen by `getActiveNavigationHref` (`src/lib/navigation-active.ts`, longest matching href). White flat topbar; solid mobile bottom bar with `text-xs` labels. PWA `themeColor` / `theme_color` #FFFFFF, `background_color` #EEF1F6, service worker cache `asset-system-pwa-v2`, `public/offline.html` recolored. Phase 2B plan 1 rebuilt the asset section of the menu (the register first, two groups, at most two levels, no repeated icon); see its section.
- Guards: `tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts` (no `bg-black/NN` in `src/components/ui`, no shadow in `getPanelClasses()`, `next/font` only in `layout.tsx`, no tone-colored or see-through focus classes, no `color` prop on `StatusBadge`, `color-scheme: only light` and no `@custom-variant dark`), `tests/navigation-active.test.ts`, and new contrast pairs in `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` (sidebar, ring and input at 3:1, `info` ≠ `primary`, `canvas` ≠ `card`). `npm test` after the last code commit: 1,617 tests, 1,616 pass, 0 fail, 1 skipped (baseline 1,590 / 1,589 / 0 / 1).
- Dev-app check (Task 8): keyboard tab walk 40/40 with visible focus; active-row ring outside the row on white; forced-colors outline solid; reduced motion stops sheet and scrim animation; table digits are tabular; toasts use Plex; axe color-contrast 0 on dashboard, assets, new asset and audit round at 1440 and 375 (1 on asset detail, see below); Thai at 125% and 200% zoom has no clipped marks. Two fixes came out of it (`fe760ae`): A4 operation documents keep `text-sm` at 20/14 through `[--text-sm--line-height:calc(20/14)]` on `.operation-print-page`, so the check-in and check-out forms print on one page again (the 22px ramp pushed the signature row to page 2), and the register status-tab nav uses `md:overflow-visible`, so the active tab underline is no longer clipped (phones keep horizontal scroll; replaced after the final review, see below).
- Final-review fixes:
  - One focus color for real: the 8 red delete/cancel controls use `focus-visible:ring-ring` instead of `focus-visible:ring-danger` (and `/40`); the guard now bans every tone (danger, success, warning, info, destructive) and any see-through `focus-visible:ring-*/NN`.
  - Register status tabs scroll horizontally at every width again (`overflow-x-auto` stays); the baseline is an inset shadow on the list (`md:shadow-[inset_0_-1px_0_var(--border)]`), so the active tab's `md:border-b-2` sits inside the scroller and is never clipped. Since 2B plan 3 the tabs are `TabNav`: both lines are inset shadows at every width, and phones show the underline instead of pills.
  - Status markers use the system text color in forced-colors mode (`forced-colors:bg-[CanvasText]`, ring `forced-colors:border-[CanvasText]`), so they stay visible on dark high-contrast themes.
  - Sidebar: the active row gets an outline in forced-colors mode; each section is `role="group"` labelled by its heading (ids prefixed `mobile-` in the drawer); `aria-current="page"` only when the path equals the entry (`isExactNavigationMatch`), `"true"` on the parent entry of a detail page.
  - `visual-foundation-guards` can no longer pass vacuously: it asserts `getPanelClasses()` is found and that more than 40 `<StatusBadge … />` call sites were scanned.
  - `globals.css` has `@source not "../../docs"` and `@source not "../../tests"`, so Tailwind no longer emits dead classes picked up from plan, spec and test regexes.
- Before/after screenshots (1440 and 375): `.superpowers/sdd/screenshots/ui-foundation/before/` and `after/` (git-ignored, on the development machine only).
- Verification: `npm run verify` passes after the final-review fixes (tests 1,618 · pass 1,617 · skipped 1 · build 60/60 pages).
- Re-check on the production build: the dev server that had been running since before Task 1 kept a stale `globals.css` (Turbopack missed the last edit on Windows), so every visual check was redone on `next start` of the final build. Shipped CSS confirmed (`color-scheme: light only`, default border #D7DEE8, primary checkboxes); after-images retaken; axe color-contrast 0 on every checked page except the pre-existing "เอกสารล่าสุด" span; tab walk 40/40; check-in A4 1,003px and checkout both print on one page. The transfer A4 document was not previewed because the dev DB has no transfer. **Restart `npm run dev` before reviewing the new look locally.**
- **Before deploy:** the thermal label preview now renders at weight 700 (Plex Sans Thai has no 900). The user must test-print labels; if they are hard to read, load Noto Sans Thai 900 on the label print page only. Installed PWAs re-download their precache once (cache `v2`).
- Deferred: the asset detail quick action "เอกสารล่าสุด" looks disabled without disabled semantics (axe flags 3.44:1; phase 2; since 2B plan 4 the disabled quick actions use the faint ink without opacity, which is AA on `background`) · dashboard and admin page-level cards still carry `shadow-sm` (phase 2/3) · the admin users table overflows at 1440 (pre-existing) · info notices, role chips and a few info icons still use the `info` tokens and appear teal (phase 2) · `tag` adoption, shared Input/Select, quiet toasts, `border-{tone}/NN` tints, remaining `text-[11px]` (phase 2) · page layouts, splitting Ready from In Use badges, login and sidebar footer (phase 3) · tab titles, 404/global-error pages, Excel header color (phase 4).
- Do not run `npx shadcn add`: it writes oklch tokens and a `.dark` block into `src/app/globals.css`. Copy components by hand and review the diff.

## UI Round 4 Phase 2A Shared Components (2026-10-11)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2a` (from `master` `081d538`; phase 1 is merged but not deployed). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-ui-phase2a-design.md` (its §10 rulings I1–I23 override the section files in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-ui-phase2a/`) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-ui-phase2a.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`. Phase 2 runs 2A → (2B list pages and page frames ∥ 2C forms and dialogs) → 2D (the rest, then the guards lock). 2A adds every token phase 2 needs, restyles the shared components in their own files (so every page that uses them changes) and runs four codemods. No page is recomposed; no API, schema, RBAC, migration or dependency change.
- **Do not deploy 2A alone (I22).** Shared fields are white with 3:1 borders, while the 282 hand-written fields that still use `border-border` keep the old look, so 16 forms show both. Ship 2A with the first 2C batch.
- **Tokens** (`src/app/globals.css`): `--faint-foreground` #56677F, `--control-border` #C3CDDA, `--border-soft` #E7EBF1, `--row-hover` #F3F6FB and `--neutral-ink` #2A4468, each with a `--color-*` line in `@theme inline`. In the plain `@theme`: `--radius-control` 6px, `--text-meta` 13/20, `--text-metric` 28/32 (500, −0.02em), `--text-count` 20/24 (600, −0.01em), `--shadow-field-focus` and `--shadow-field-error`. Utilities `select-chevron` and the new `tag` (weight 400, `user-select: all`). A base `::placeholder` rule gives every field the faint ink; the 13 per-field placeholder colors are gone.
- **Know these before you use the new pieces:**
  - `cn()` (`src/lib/utils.ts`) is built on `extendTailwindMerge`, so `text-meta|metric|count`, `rounded-control` and `shadow-overlay|field-focus|field-error` merge correctly (`tests/class-merge.test.ts`). Always merge with `cn()`, never with `twMerge` directly. Tailwind 4.2.4 builds no CSS for a class whose token is missing, and it reports nothing.
  - **No new tokens after 2A (I21).** 2B and 2C run in parallel: neither adds a custom property to `:root`, `@theme inline` or `@theme`, and neither edits `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` (I4). If a token is really needed, land it as a small change on `master` before branching.
  - **Ratchet guards** in `tests/visual-foundation-guards.test.ts` (R1, R2 and, since 2B plan 5, R7 use `assertWithinBudget`, built on `countMatchesByFile` in `tests/helpers/source-files.ts`; R3 uses `findMatches`): R1 tone opacity (none: the budget is `{}` since 2B plan 2, when the selected tab's count chip of `audit/rounds/page.tsx` went with `TabNav`), R7 neutral fills (one test over every line, table headers included, since 2B plan 5; `TABLE_HEADER_FILLS` keeps 1 fill in 1 file, the import preview header, for 2D; it was 44 → 29 → 17 → 11 → 9 → 1 over 2B plans 1–4, 2C plan 4 and 2B plan 5), R2 shadows (10 in 9 files), R3 text under 12px (none outside `asset-label-print.tsx`). A budget means "at most N in this file": lower a number in the task that removes a site, never raise one. 2D sets every budget to `{}`.
  - Fields take `fieldSize` (`md` 36 · `lg` 40, the default · `xl` 48), never `size`.
  - `Button busy` uses `aria-disabled`: never pass `disabled` with it (focus drops to `<body>`), never `asChild` (type error), and only from client state (a server file cannot hand over the click guard).
  - `DropdownMenuItem unavailable` keeps a blocked item focusable and readable with its reason (`DropdownMenuItemText` + `DropdownMenuItemDescription`); it cannot be combined with `asChild`.
  - `Notice` needs `tone`, and the caller writes `role="alert"`, `role="status"` or `aria-live` itself.
  - `StatusBadge` has no `size` prop any more (one 22px size).
- **What changed, by area** (in `src/components/ui/` unless noted):
  - Buttons: `buttonVariants` variants `default | outline | ghost | danger | destructive | warning` (no `secondary`, no `link`) and sizes `sm | md | lg | default | icon-sm | icon` (`default` = `lg`, 40px, until 2D); `Button busy`; `linkVariants`; `getSafeActionLinkClasses()` rebuilt on `buttonVariants`; `getTouchIconButtonClasses` removed; `ActionButton` and `getActionButtonClasses` deprecated (2D deletes them). The back and edit links in the asset detail header use `buttonVariants`.
  - Fields: `field-variants.ts` (`fieldControlVariants`, `textareaClasses`, `fieldReadOnlyClasses`, `checkboxVariants`), `Input`, `NativeSelect`, `Textarea`, `Field` (`FieldLabel`, `FieldHint`, `FieldError`) and `SearchField`. Wired in only at `getFieldControlClasses()` (`src/lib/design-system.ts`), `SearchableSelect`, `ScannerTextInput` and `FileDropzone`.
  - Menus: dropdown, popover and command restyled; `unavailable` items; `Kbd`; `CommandDialog` removed; the topbar language menu is a radio group without flags. New messages `nav.openMenu`, `nav.toggleSidebar`, `nav.languageMenu`, `nav.userMenu`, `nav.closeMenu` and `globalSearch.keyboardSelect` / `keyboardOpen` / `keyboardClose` (replacing `globalSearch.keyboardHint`).
  - Badges: one size and a `neutral-ink` marker; the `size` attribute was removed from 52 call sites in 19 files.
  - Breadcrumbs: `items: { label, href?, code? }[]`, `common.breadcrumb`, `code: true` on 8 pages. `Nil` / `orNil` (`common.noValue`) and `src/lib/display-format.ts` (`isBlankValue`, `formatCount`, `formatMoney`; `common.baht`).
  - Feedback: `AppToaster` (replaces `<Toaster richColors />`), `toneIcons`, `ActionEmptyState` without its dashed box (new prop `actionVariant`), `EmptyNote` and `Notice`. The stale state of `DataFreshnessBanner` is the first `Notice` and the first `<Button busy>`.
  - Built but not used yet in 2A (their API may still change until 2D locks the guards): `Textarea`, `SearchField`, `EmptyNote`, `Nil` / `orNil`, `display-format`, `linkVariants`, the `danger` variant and `checkboxVariants`.
- **Codemods** (one commit each; the scripts stay out of the repo): R1 `border-{tone}/NN` → `border-{tone}-border` (254 sites in 71 files, plus 13 lines fixed by hand); R7 translucent neutral fills → solid tokens (80 sites in 36 files; the register's sticky cells are no longer see-through on row hover); R2 `shadow-sm` removed (232 sites in 84 files, 6 kept; the 2 segmented controls now mark the chosen option with `primary-soft`, weight 600 and 6px buttons); R3 `text-[11px]` / `text-[10px]` → `text-xs` (10 sites in 7 files).
- **Tests:** new files `tests/class-merge.test.ts`, `button-busy-ui.test.ts`, `field-primitives.test.ts`, `menu-primitives-ui.test.ts`, `breadcrumbs-ui.test.ts`, `display-format.test.ts` and `feedback-states-ui.test.ts`. New guards in `visual-foundation-guards.test.ts`: one placeholder color; no `text-[13px]`, `text-[28px]` or `rounded-[6px]`; `num` beside `text-metric` / `text-count`; `tag` never bold or resized; no faint text on a status fill; R1, R7, R2 and R3; no emoji in `src` or `messages`.
- Verification: `npm run verify` passes (tests 1,693 · pass 1,692 · skipped 1 · build 60/60 pages).
- Dev-app check (Task 12): checked on `next start` of the production build at 1440 and 375px (700 and 768px for asset new and the register) in Thai, with one pass in English, with the keyboard, axe color-contrast, forced colors and reduced motion. axe color-contrast found 0 violations on the dashboard, the register with a row menu open, asset new, settings, the settings label tab and check-in with an error toast open. The error toast has the danger-soft fill and border, 12px corners, the overlay shadow, sits 72px from the top, uses IBM Plex, and its region and close button are named "การแจ้งเตือน" and "ปิด"; an `unavailable` menu item is reached by the arrow keys, its reason is part of its text and Enter keeps the menu open and does nothing; the chosen segmented option is primary-soft, weight 600, 6px corners; breadcrumbs carry `aria-label="เส้นทางหน้า"` in the server HTML and the current code is Plex Mono 500 with `user-select: all`; `StatusBadge` is 22px with 13/20 text and 6px corners; the busy refresh button keeps focus with `aria-busy` and `aria-disabled` and shows the spinner. Forced colors keep the focus outline, badge markers use CanvasText and `<select>` shows the system arrow; under reduced motion menus do not animate. `ScannerTextInput` field and camera button are 40px at 1440 and 44px (16px text) at 375; `FileDropzone` shows the teal outline on Tab; A4 check-in and check-out documents still print on 1 page; Thai text in badges, meta text and breadcrumbs is not clipped at 200% zoom; English topbar names come from `nav.*`. Seen but not caused by 2A, left for later: attachment thumbnails return 500 on the dev machine (files not present locally, as before 2A), the signature pad's "ล้างลายเซ็น" button wraps at 375px (2C), and at 200% zoom the topbar search placeholder runs under the notification bell. Accepted until 2C (I22): `SearchableSelect` labels are 13px beside hand-written 14px labels. No fixes were needed. ## UI Round 4 Phase 2C Plan 1 Forms and Dialogs (2026-10-11)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2c` (from `master` `5ffee76` with the P0 commits; 2A is merged but not deployed). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-ui-phase2c-design.md` (its §10 rulings I1–I36 override the section files in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-ui-phase2c/`) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-11-ui-phase2c-plan1.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`.
  - 2C moves forms and dialogs onto the 2A primitives in five batches (plans 1–5).
  - Plan 1 is:
    - P0: shared pieces that 2B needs too
    - 2C-0: new primitives
    - the ZodError fix
    - batch 1: 26 files, 179 fields
  - No workflow, page, schema, RBAC, migration, dependency or token change. The only server change is the validation message.
- **Deploy:** plan 1 ships together with 2A and the other commits not deployed since `ab1b873` (56 at `5ffee76`), as one deploy that the user starts after the dev-app check and the manual checks below. On 2026-10-12 the user moved this deploy to the end of phase 2; see the plan 2 section.
  - No schema change, so `ab1b873` stays a clean rollback.
  - The P0 commits are also on local `master` (fast-forward after Task 3, not pushed). `master` with P0 must not be deployed on its own (2A I22).
- **P0** (in `src/components/` unless noted; 2B builds on these):
  - `ui/segmented.tsx` (`Segmented`) on the label-name setting and the audit room-list tabs.
  - `labelAction` on `Field`, plus `fieldLabelClasses` (`ui/field-variants.ts`) and `FieldRequiredMark` (`ui/field.tsx`) for `<legend>`.
  - `open()`, `hideTrigger` and `returnFocusRef` on `DisposalDecisionButton`, `DisposalExecutionButton` and `AuditMarkNotFoundButton`, so 2B's row menus can open these dialogs (`tests/dialog-trigger-handles.test.ts`).
- **2C-0 primitives** (in `src/components/ui/` unless noted):
  - Dialogs:
    - `AccessibleDialogBody`, `AccessibleDialogFooter`, `AccessibleDialogCancel` and `accessibleDialogFormClasses`
    - the ghost X named "ปิด" in `dialog.tsx` and `sheet.tsx`
    - white sheets with a header line and the bottom safe area
    - the `AlertDialogFooter` order
  - Files and scanning: `FileDropzone` `description`, `FileQueueList`, and `aria-label` and `disabled` on `ScannerTextInput`.
  - Choices, steps and sections: `ChoiceCard` / `ChoiceChip` / `preventEnterSubmit`, `Steps` with `src/lib/step-states.ts`, `FormSection`, and a quiet `FormContextBanner`.
  - Save bar: `StickyFormFooter`.
- **Know these before you touch a form or dialog:**
  - Keep `required`, `maxLength` and `minLength` on the control inside `Field` (K4). `Field required` adds only the star and `aria-required`. No form validates with zod on the client yet, so these attributes and the existing toasts are the only checks in many forms. Plan 1 changes no validation behavior.
  - `busy={x}` on the button that runs, and `x` never in its `disabled` (focus would drop to `<body>`). The label stays the same while busy (K13). The busy-label message keys stay unused until 2D deletes them.
  - Dialog footers: `AccessibleDialogCancel` first, the primary action last, no `className` and no `size=`. A `<form className={accessibleDialogFormClasses}>` wraps `AccessibleDialogBody` and `AccessibleDialogFooter`.
  - A dialog that renders inside another `<form>` (the settings prefix dialog) has no `<form>` of its own and no `type="submit"`. React sends `submit` up the component tree through the portal (guard GD5).
  - `StickyFormFooter` only on focus-task routes, as the last child of the `<form>`.
  - `ChoiceCard`/`ChoiceChip` keep the input visible and stop Enter from submitting; a hand-written checkbox row uses `preventEnterSubmit`.
  - **Guard file `tests/form-migration-guards.test.ts`** (GF fields, GB buttons and busy, GD dialogs, GL save bar and bottom navigation; JSX reader in `tests/helpers/source-files.ts`):
    - It holds per-file budgets for the 66 files of 2C.
    - The commit that migrates a file lowers that file's budgets and adds it to `MIGRATED_FILES`; never raise a number.
    - `CONSTRAINT_FLOORS` (the `required`/`maxLength`/`minLength` counts) never go down.
    - 2B does not edit this file.
  - **Never run a formatter** on 2C files (spec §9.8). Several tests pin attribute order and line breaks, and some read CRLF files without converting them, so a regex with `\n` breaks silently.
  - No new tokens (2A I21 still holds).
- **ZodError fix (spec §8.5):**
  - `errorResponse` (`src/lib/api-response.ts`) and the transaction-cancellation response answer every `ZodError` with status 400 and `{ error: "Invalid request data", code: "VALIDATION_FAILED" }` (`validationFailedBody` in `src/lib/api-error-exposure.ts`). They used to send the JSON of every zod issue.
  - The catalog maps both strings to `apiErrors.validationFailed`: "ข้อมูลที่กรอกไม่ครบหรือไม่ถูกต้อง ตรวจสอบแล้วลองอีกครั้ง".
  - Routes are unchanged. V track plan 1 added `fieldErrors` to this body (see "V Track Plan 1" below).
- **Batch 1:**
  - 1a: PM plan form, repair record form, the 3 repair dialogs, and the select and drop zone of repair attachments.
  - 1b: check-out, check-in, transfer, bulk move, `SignaturePad`, `OperationReviewDialog`.
  - 1c: disposal request and batch forms, the decision and execution dialogs, the asset picker.
  - 1d: audit check sheet, the finding review dialogs, not found, missing component.
  - 1g: asset model form.
  - 1e:
    - asset form: `StickyFormFooter`, `labelAction` on status and condition, photo-type chips
    - batch form: column chips, labelled cells
    - component install: `Steps`, and the back button reads "กลับ"
    - asset attachments
  - 1f:
    - the bottom navigation hidden on 25 routes (K15)
    - settings: `StickyFormFooter`, `FormSection`, the prefix dialog
    - depreciation policy builder: group chips
- **What users see in batch 1:**
  - Fields are white with 3:1 borders. On desktop, 44px fields and buttons are now 40px (K7); phones stay at least 44px with 16px text.
  - Disabled buttons are grey instead of faded. Busy buttons keep focus and their label.
  - Dialog footers are always visible, with Cancel on top on phones. The X reads "ปิด".
  - The asset save bar no longer shifts 192px when the sidebar collapses (G11).
  - The bottom navigation is gone on the settings page and 24 new/edit form routes, and back on the PM and disposal-batch lists.
  - A validation error reads in Thai instead of JSON.
  - Sheets elsewhere (2B's register filter and row ⋯ sheets, the drawers) are white with a header line.
- **Messages:**
  - 26 new keys:
    - `common`: `removeFile`, `selectedFiles`, `stepOf`, `stepComplete`, `stepCurrent`, `stepUpcoming`, `stepRejected`
    - `apiErrors.validationFailed`
    - `repairRecord`: `dropFilesTitle`, `dropFilesHint`
    - `checkin.photoAfterDescription`
    - `disposalPage`: `saleValueBaht`, `salvageValueBaht`, `actualSaleValueBaht`, `actualSalvageValueBaht`, `requestEvidenceDescription`
    - `auditScan.dropAuditPhotoDescription`
    - `asset`: `purchasePriceBaht`, `purchaseDocumentAmountBaht`, `purchaseDocumentDate`, `photoType`, `checklistPhotoDescription`, `componentInstallSteps`, `componentStepIdentify`, `componentStepDetails`, `componentStepReview`
  - 3 keys removed: `checkin.photoQueued`, `checkin.removePhoto`, `auditScan.dropAuditPhotoSelected`.
- **Tests:**
  - New files: `tests/dialog-trigger-handles.test.ts`, `file-evidence-ui.test.ts`, `step-states.test.ts`, `steps-ui.test.ts`, `choice-controls-ui.test.ts`, `sticky-form-footer-ui.test.ts`, `asset-forms-ui.test.ts` and `form-migration-guards.test.ts`.
  - Additions in `field-primitives`, `accessible-dialog`, `sheet-drawers-ui`, `scanner-text-input`, `api-error-exposure`, `api-error-catalog` and `mobile-field-navigation`, and call-site tests appended to `pm-plans-ui`, `repair-record-ui`, `repair-record-detail-ui`, `maintenance-attachments-ui`, `asset-handover-checkin`, `asset-handover-checkout`, `legacy-checkin-flow` and `asset-operation-confirmation-ui`.

- Verification: `npm run verify` passes (tests 1,802 · pass 1,801 · skipped 1 · build 60/60 pages).
- Dev-app check (Task 21): checked on `next start` of the production build at 1440 and 375px (all batch-1 forms), 1024px (G11, sidebar open and collapsed) and 375/768px (G11b), in Thai, with axe (color-contrast, label, aria and button-name rules), forced colors, reduced motion and the Enter key in the settings prefix dialog. Results: G11's asset-new save bar spans `main` at 1024px with the sidebar open (left 256px) and collapsed (left 64px), stays at the viewport bottom and no longer shifts 192px; G11b's settings save bar was covered by 49px at 375 and 41px at 768 before K15 and is now fully visible while scrolling. At 375, bottom navigation is hidden on settings and the asset-model form and remains on the PM, disposal-batch and asset lists. Axe found 0 violations on each of 10 pages (settings, asset new, checkout, check-in, PM new, repair new, disposal new, disposal batch new, asset-model form, audit scan), including the nested-interactive rule. The prefix dialog has no `<form>` or submit button, its X reads "ปิด", and Enter sent no settings request and showed no toast. Saving a branch with code "   " returned 400 with `{ error: "Invalid request data", code: "VALIDATION_FAILED" }`; the toast read "ข้อมูลที่กรอกไม่ครบหรือไม่ถูกต้อง ตรวจสอบแล้วลองอีกครั้ง" with detail "Invalid request data", with no JSON. Repair radio cards showed the chosen option in primary-soft, the phone asset save bar stacked Cancel above Save, and the signature clear button stayed on one line. The 9 dialogs awaiting batches 3–5 still have their title 4px left of their body on phones; forms outside batch 1 still use old fields and move in plans 2–4. Fixes: `d937777` added `content-start` to `Field` and `SearchableSelect` so stretched grid rows pack their contents at the top; the repair date control's label gap fell from 54px to 6px, and after the rebuild every batch-1 `Field` kept its control within 12px of its label. ## UI Round 4 Phase 2C Plan 2 Master-Data Forms (2026-10-12)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2c-plan2` (from `master` `4daf0b9`: 2A and 2C plan 1 merged and pushed, not deployed). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-ui-phase2c-design.md` §8.9 batch 2 (its §10 rulings override the section files) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-ui-phase2c-plan2.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`.
  - Batch 2 is the 8 master-data forms: branch, brand, category, company, department, employee, location and supplier (46 fields, 9 checkboxes, 26 buttons). One commit per form.
  - No new primitive, rule, message key, workflow, page, schema, RBAC, migration, dependency or token. No server change.
- **Deploy:** on 2026-10-12 the user decided to deploy once, after all of phase 2 is done (2C plans 2–5, 2B, 2D and the V track), instead of once per batch as spec §8.9 planned. Until then `master` holds 2A, 2C plan 1 and this plan undeployed, and `ab1b873` stays the rollback point (no schema change).
- **What changed in each form** (`src/components/master-data/`):
  - Every field is the shared `Field` with `Input`, `NativeSelect` or `Textarea`. Each form's local `Field` helper is gone. `required`, `maxLength`, `type`, `inputMode` and `autoComplete` stay on the controls (K4), and textareas resize vertically (`resize-y` from `Textarea`).
  - The "active" checkbox (and the category "required field" checkbox) use `checkboxVariants()`. The category "required field" row and the supplier "active" row are 44px tap targets (`min-h-11`).
  - Save is a `Button` with `busy={saving}`: the spinner replaces the save icon, the label stays, and focus stays on the button. The branch and location forms still keep Save disabled while there is no company or no branch to choose. Cancel is a link styled `buttonVariants({ variant: "outline" })`; the supplier Cancel still asks before dropping unsaved changes.
  - Category template editors: the add, move and duplicate buttons are `outline` `md`, remove custom field is `danger` `md`, and the 4 photo-item buttons are `outline` `icon` with a neutral remove icon (K22). The photo-row grid sizes its icon columns to the button (`repeat(4,auto)`), and each photo item field has a hidden label.
  - Company: the help lines under the code and asset tag code fields are `Field hint=`, so screen readers read each with its field.
  - Supplier: the 6 errors under fields come from `Field error=` with `id="supplier-<field>"`, so the error ids stay `supplier-<field>-error`. `Field` sets `aria-invalid` and `aria-describedby`, and `FieldError` has `role="alert"`.
  - The header back links stay as they were (2B, O16), as do the form panels (K28, 2D) and the footer rows.
- **Tests:**
  - New: `tests/branch-form-ui.test.ts`, `brand-form-ui`, `category-form-ui`, `company-form-ui`, `department-form-ui`, `employee-form-ui` and `location-form-ui`.
  - `tests/supplier-form-ui.test.ts`: the inline-error test now checks the 6 `Field error=` sites and the 6 field ids (spec §4.16), and one test pins the busy save and the outline cancel.
  - `tests/form-migration-guards.test.ts`: the 8 forms are in `MIGRATED_FILES` (34 files), their budgets are gone, and `CONSTRAINT_FLOORS` is unchanged.
- Verification: `npm run verify` passes (tests 1,812 · pass 1,811 · skipped 1 · build 60/60 pages).
- Dev-app check (Task 9): checked all 8 forms on `next start` of the production build at 1440, 768, 720px (200% zoom) and 375px, in Thai plus an English pass. Results: fields and Save/Cancel were 40px on desktop and 44px on phones; labels and required states matched, textareas resized vertically, and no horizontal overflow or visual regression was found. Supplier duplicate-code retries sent one POST, kept Save's label and focus during busy and after the rejection, and showed the linked inline alert; the branch whitespace-code rejection showed a Thai toast without JSON. Keyboard order, return navigation, forced colors and reduced motion passed; axe found 0 violations on all 8 forms and the supplier error state. No test data was written. Fixes: none. Open: NVDA, real iPhone/iPad checks and a successful save.
- Before/after screenshots: `.superpowers/sdd/screenshots/ui-phase2c-plan2/before/` and `after/` (git-ignored, on the development machine only).

## UI Round 4 Phase 2B Plan 1 Primitives, Master-Data Lists and Page Headers (2026-10-13)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2b` (from `master` `d38a0ee`: 2A and 2C plans 1–2 merged, not deployed). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-ui-phase2b-design.md` (its §10 rulings I1–I34 override the section files in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-ui-phase2b/`) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-12-ui-phase2b-plan1.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`.
  - Plan 2B-1 builds the list and page-frame primitives, moves the 8 master-data lists and the user list onto them, rebuilds the asset section of the sidebar, and gives every form header one back button. One commit per migrated page or form, except Task 22 (5 commits by group).
  - No schema, migration, API, RBAC, dependency or token. The one server change is the read-only pagination query of the departments list (`findMany` + `count` in one `Promise.all`; no index).
- **Pipeline (spec §8.1, replaces "plans 3–5 start after 2B merges" above):** 2B runs in the main checkout, one plan at a time: 2B-1 → 2B-2 → 2B-3 → 2B-4 → 2B-5. 2C runs in a worktree: plan 3 starts when 2B-2 has merged, plan 4 when 2B-3 and 2C-3 have merged, plan 5 when 2B-4 and 2C-4 have merged. 2D starts when 2B-5 and 2C-5 have merged, and the V track after 2D. Merges are fast-forward only; the branch that merges second rebases (DESIGN.md, VFG, `messages/*.json`, this file, the changelog, `docs/14_UI_UX_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` and `.impeccable/design.json` may conflict; any other code conflict means a fence leaked, so stop and revert).
- **Deploy:** none in 2B. The user decided on 2026-10-12 to deploy once, after all of phase 2 and the user's checks on real devices; `ab1b873` stays the rollback point (no schema change).
- **New primitives** (`src/components/ui/`; all server-safe except `row-actions.tsx` and `data-table-scroll.tsx`; rules in `DESIGN.md` §5):
  - `page-header.tsx`: `PageHeader` (`title`, `meta`, `actions`, `back`, `breadcrumbs`, `compactOnMobile`, `className`) and `PageBackLink` (`href`, `label`, `onClick`). No hooks, so client forms render them. `back` is a node, so each page keeps its literal `href={returnToHref}`.
  - `data-table.tsx`: `DataTable`, `DataTableHeader`, `DataTableHead` (`sort`, `aria-sort`), `DataTableBody`, `DataTableRow`, `DataTableCell`, `DataTableFoot`, `TableEmptyRow`, and the exported class strings for tables that cannot move whole. One density (I3). `data-table-scroll.tsx`: `DataTableScrollArea`, the overflow hint (`common.tableScrollHint`). `ClickableTableRow` now uses the shared row classes (`group/row`, lit while focus is inside or its menu is open).
  - `pagination.tsx`: the range (`common.showingRange`), the page sizes 25/50/100 (`common.pageSizeLabel`) and `aria-live`; words-only buttons. `src/lib/list-pagination.ts`: `LIST_PAGE_SIZES`, `parseListPage`, `listPageWindow`, `lastListPage`, `appendListPage`.
  - `filter-select.tsx` (`FilterSelect`), `filter-chips.tsx` (`FilterChipList`, `FilterChipItem`), and `FilterFormActions` in `filter-panel.tsx`.
  - `row-actions.tsx`: `RowActions` (ช1: next step + ⋯; a menu from `md`, a bottom sheet below; `deleteAction` uses the same hook and confirm as `MasterDataDeleteButton`; `common.moreActionsFor`). `dropdown-menu.tsx` exports `menuItemClasses` and `menuItemDestructiveClasses`.
  - `tab-nav.tsx` (`TabNav`, with `onSelect` for 2C plan 5's settings tabs), `stat-strip.tsx` (`StatStrip`), `key-value-list.tsx` (`KeyValueList`) and `ContentPanel divided` / `flush` / `ContentPanelRows` in `content-panel.tsx`.
- **Pages and forms moved:**
  - The 8 master-data lists (`/master-data/{branches,brands,categories,companies,departments,employees,locations,suppliers}`, with `supplier-list-view.tsx`) and `/admin/users`: `PageHeader`, filter panels on `FilterSelect` and `FilterFormActions`, `DataTable` with `TableEmptyRow`, `Pagination` with 25/50/100 and a redirect past the last page, `StatStrip` instead of the summary tiles, `RowActions` ("แก้ไข" + ⋯ with "ลบ"; users has "แก้ไข" only), and `formatCount`, `Nil` and `tag`. The 9 `*-delete-button.tsx` wrappers are deleted; `master-data-delete-button.tsx` stays without callers until 2D. Departments now pages on the server (B4). The brand picker stays a vertical list with `aria-current` (I11).
  - Form headers: the 9 master-data forms, `admin-user-form`, `role-permission-form` (only its header: the create page now reads "เพิ่มบทบาท", I32), `asset-form`, `asset-component-manager` (its header no longer sticks), `/disposal/new`, `/disposal/batch/new`, `/maintenance/new`, `/maintenance/pm/new` and `/maintenance/pm/[id]/edit` use `PageHeader` with `PageBackLink`. The three maintenance pages read "กลับ" instead of "ยกเลิก", and the PM edit title is the plan's name with its code in the meta.
  - The 2C operation forms (`checkin`, `checkout` through `OperationShell`, `transfer`, `bulk-move`), `audit-round-form` and `asset-batch-form` draw their titles with `PageHeader` (I13). The settings tables and the asset batch tables have the white header; the depreciation builder formats through `formatCount` and `formatMoney` (R-e, R-f).
- **Changed before their own plan** (the shared parts moved under them): `MasterDataHeader` is a thin wrapper of `PageHeader` (the asset register, findings and rounds headers); `MasterDataSearch` is a frameless `SearchField` + outline `Button` toolbar on 6 pages; the register's `Pagination` lost its arrows; every `ClickableTableRow` row (19 in 18 files) stays lit while focus is inside. The findings "กลับไปรอบตรวจนับ" button still shows the plus icon until 2B-2 (plan 2B-2 made it an outline back button with a left arrow).
- **Sidebar (B1):** "ทะเบียนทรัพย์สิน" is the first item of the asset section (one click to `/[locale]/assets`); the groups จัดการทรัพย์สิน and ธุรกรรม follow; at most two levels; 39 entries with 39 different icons. `nav.assetRegistryGroup` is deleted and `SidebarGroup` has no `depth`.
- **Messages:** new `common.tableScrollHint`, `common.showingRange`, `common.pageSizeLabel`, `common.moreActionsFor`; deleted `nav.assetRegistryGroup`.
- **Guards:**
  - VFG R7 is a budget per file (`TABLE_HEADER_FILLS`): 44 in 31 files at `d38a0ee`, 29 in 20 after this plan. R1 (1) and R2 (10 in 9 files) are unchanged.
  - **Guard file `tests/list-frame-guards.test.ts`** (LFG, 19 counters LH1…LR1 plus L0 and a sample test; JSX reader in `tests/helpers/source-files.ts`):
    - `BUDGETS` started at the `d38a0ee` counts (spec §10.4 table C) plus two LB1 rows `0 → 1` for the back items of the mobile action bars of `audit/rounds/[id]` and `maintenance/[id]`.
    - The commit that moves a site lowers that file's rows, and the commit that finishes a file adds it to `MIGRATED_LIST_FILES`; L0 then holds the file to `KEEP`. Never raise a number.
    - `KEEP` is what may stay after 2B, each row with its reason. Plan 2B-5 sets `BUDGETS` to `KEEP` (test L9); 2D sets every budget to `{}`.
    - 2C never needs to edit this file (its budgets are maxima). A new assertion in a test that reads a CRLF file without converting it has no `\n`.

## UI Round 4 Phase 2B Plan 2 Disposal, Audit, Maintenance, Data-Quality and Integration Lists (2026-10-13)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2b-plan2` (from `master` `e3f20ce`: 2B plan 1 merged, not deployed). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-ui-phase2b-design.md` (its §10 rulings I1–I34 override the section files in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-ui-phase2b/`) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-13-ui-phase2b-plan2.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`.
  - Plan 2B-2 moves the 18 files of spec §8.6 onto the 2B-1 primitives (the disposal queue, its batches and a batch's page; audit findings, rounds, a round's detail and its pending list; the repair list and the PM plans; the data-quality page and its review queue; the integrations header) and lets the PM edit page return to the list page it came from (spec §5.9.2 row 6; `maintenance-plan-form.tsx`, a migrated 2C batch 1a file, gets one optional prop while no 2C plan is open). One commit per file; a page goes with its new wrapper, helper or deleted pager, and Task 14 is one commit across the return section, the edit page and the form.
  - No new primitive, schema, migration, API, RBAC, dependency, index or token. The server changes are read-only queries in the pages (`findMany`, `count`, `groupBy`, one `findUnique`).
- **2C plan 3 may start once this branch merges** (spec B7, §8.1): 2B is done with the three batch-3 files it shares with plan 3, `disposal-bulk-approval.tsx`, `disposal-bulk-execution.tsx` and `audit-findings-batch-actions.tsx` ("Handed on to 2C plan 3" below gives their state).
- **Deploy:** none, as for 2B plan 1: one deploy after all of phase 2; `ab1b873` stays the rollback point (no schema change).
- **Real pages (B4, 25/50/100, 25 by default, a page past the end redirects to the last page):**
  - `/audit/findings` (it showed the first 200): `findMany` with `skip`/`take` and `orderBy` `reportedAt desc, id asc`. The total is the strip count for the four counted queues and one `count({ where })` for approved, rejected, exception and all; the round chip reads its code with one `findUnique`. The exports still take the whole filtered set.
  - `/audit/rounds/[id]/pending` (it showed 200): the items load apart from the round, `auditItem.findMany` + `count` in one `Promise.all` after the read-only check; the count box counts every pending item.
  - `/disposal/batches` (it showed the newest 100): `findMany` + `count`.
  - `/maintenance/pm` (it showed up to 500): `groupBy` by `planState`, then one `findMany` window per state from `planStatePageSlices`, so the order stays active → paused → ended, then `nextDueDate`.
  - `/audit/rounds` (unbounded): the query stays whole apart from the `id` tie-break; the filtered rounds are cut in memory, and a page past the end clamps (I21).
  - `returnTo` carries the page and the page size: rounds → round detail and scan, batches → a batch's page (`normalizeDisposalBatchReturnTo`), PM → the edit page (section `"maintenance-pm"`). `/disposal` and `/maintenance` already paged; they gained the redirect past the last page. A round's results keep their own params, moved to `Pagination` and redirect past their last page too.
- **Pages moved:**
  - `/disposal`: `PageHeader` · the stages as `TabNav` with th-TH counts · one `DisposalFilterForm` (in the phone `<details>`, whose summary now reads "ใช้ตัวกรอง N รายการ", and in `FilterPanel` from `md`) · `ContentPanel divided flush` with `DataTable` · `RowActions` through `DisposalRowActions`: the next step "พิจารณาคำขอ" or "บันทึกตัดจำหน่าย" opens the existing 2C dialog through its handle, "ดู" opens the request, ⋯ holds "เปิดคำขอ" and "เปิดใบงานชุด". The next steps carry no icon so their words fit the 120px slot; the row uses the short key `disposalPage.executeDisposalShort` ("บันทึกตัดจำหน่าย" / "Dispose", approved by the user 2026-10-13) because "บันทึกตัดจำหน่ายจริง" is 115px at 13px, while the phone card and the dialog keep the full words. The money line under the type is `formatMoney` with the unit; `Pagination` sits once under the cards and the table. The bulk approval and bulk execution lists (Tasks 1–2): the row and page checkboxes on `checkboxVariants` in 44px labels, the toolbar buttons on `Button`, "ตรวจสอบและอนุมัติ" with `busy`.

The button that starts the work stays in place with `busy` and its label, so focus does not drop.

The submit button keeps `busy` through the preview and through the commit, and the footer's "กำลังบันทึก" line went (the body's `aria-live` line still says it). Cancel cancels a running preview and is grey during the commit. With no eligible request, the sentence moved from the button to `<Notice tone="warning" role="alert">` in the body, and the button reads "ยืนยันดำเนินการ 0 รายการ" and cannot be pressed (spec §5.7). `closeLabel` stays: while the preview runs, the X reads "ยกเลิกการตรวจสอบ". ## UI Round 4 Phase 2B Plan 3 Asset Register (2026-10-13)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2b-plan3` (from `master` `886a290`: 2B plans 1–2 merged, not deployed), in the main checkout beside 2C plan 3 in `.worktrees/ui-phase2c-plan3`.   - Plan 2B-3 moves the 14 files of spec §8.7 onto the 2B-1 primitives: the register page and its toolbar, search field, filter sheet body, status tabs, chips, sort, column and export menus, table, phone list and row actions; the column widths in `src/lib/asset-register-columns.ts`; and the header of the import/export page. One commit per file.
  - No new primitive, schema, migration, API, RBAC, dependency, index or token, and no server change: the register's queries, its URL filters, `AssetRegisterNavigationProvider` and its row model are untouched.
- **2C plan 4 may start once this branch and 2C plan 3 have both merged** (spec B7, §8.1): 2B is done with the three files it shares with plan 4, `asset-register-table.tsx`, `D/assets/page.tsx` and `D/asset-management/import-export/page.tsx` ("Handed on to 2C plan 4" below gives their state).
- **Deploy:** none, as for 2B plans 1–2: one deploy after all of phase 2; `ab1b873` stays the rollback point (no schema change).
- **The register contract holds** (`DESIGN.md` asset register pattern; spec §7.3, I3, I4, I26):

The operations preset (the default) goes 1,094 → 1,116 and accounting 1,098 → 1,116, within the 1,117px a 1440px screen leaves; audit (1,276) and all (1,632) scroll as before. The 172px column holds the 120px next step and ⋯ between `pl-1.5 pr-2` (157.5 ≥ 156). Measured at 1440 and 1366: the operations preset, the accounting preset and the default (no saved preset) overflow 0px at 1440 with the actions column at 172px and rows at 57px (operations before: 0px, 150px and 57px; accounting and the default overflowed 0px before), and audit overflows 151px (before 129px); at 1366 operations, accounting and the default overflow 65px (before 43, 47 and 43px) and audit 225px (before 203px).

  - The key and actions columns stay pinned, with a line on their inner edge instead of the edge shadow (R-j). The table no longer imports `hasRemainingHorizontalContent`; `src/lib/horizontal-scroll.ts` stays for `asset-detail-tabs.tsx`. The register keeps its own scroll hint, `isOverflowing` + `asset.tableScrollHint` (I4).
- **What changed, file by file:**
  - `D/assets/page.tsx`: `PageHeader compactOnMobile className="mb-4 md:mb-6"` with the create link (`buttonVariants({ size: "md" })` and `Plus`) as its action (I32); the tab counts through `formatCount`. `MasterDataHeader` has no caller left; plan 2B-5 deletes it.
  - Toolbar: no card from `md` (the sticky phone bar is unchanged), the three scope selects are `FilterSelect hideLabel fieldSize="md"` (their `key`, `defaultValue` and `onChange` kept), and ⚙ is `Button variant="outline" size="md"`. Search: `SearchField` with `fieldSize="md"`, `loading`, `onClear` and `clearLabel`; the 400ms debounce, the IME handling, Enter and the GET form without JavaScript are unchanged.
  - Filter sheet (its body only; `ui/sheet.tsx` is untouched): seven `FilterSelect`s keyed by filter name and value (`` key={`status-${filters.statusId}`} ``, because an unset filter is the same empty value in every sibling), status and condition with their help button in `labelAction` (24px), and "แสดง N รายการ" `busy` while the count loads. `function LabeledSelect` is gone, and the call no longer passes `closeLabel` or `pr-14`.

The sort trigger is `ghost` below `md` (a frame would touch the lines of the 44px summary bar) and takes the outline colors from `md` through `md:` classes, `md:active:*` included, because Tailwind 4.2.4 emits `md:bg-surface` after `active:bg-muted`; a change to the `ghost` or `outline` variant must update them.

Measured at 375 (the gap between the last content and the bottom bar, before → after): the employee and supplier pages went from a large empty band to about 24px between the last card and the bottom navigation, with nothing under the bar; the my-asset page was not reachable with the dev data ("Still open" below); the asset page's bottom bar and its padding are unchanged (O11).

The back button is `PageBackLink` ("กลับ", or "กลับไปสแกน" from the scanner; `href={returnToHref}` kept), then ⋯ (`AssetDetailActionMenu`, its props unchanged and still self-closing), then "แก้ไข" (`md`, from `md`; phones reach it in ⋯). The seven fact groups are `KeyValueList`s (`function Info` is gone; an empty custodian reads `asset.noCustodian`); each tab's `SummaryStrip` is a `StatStrip` (numbers as values, the sentences in the foot line, `attention` for an open repair or an audit result to follow up); the repair and audit history tables are `DataTable framed={false}` with a caption and the scroll hint, the repair status is a `StatusBadge` and the scan count `formatCount`, right-aligned; money is `formatMoney` with "บาท" (the purchase document line of the timeline takes the document's currency); the disabled quick actions drop `opacity-70` for the faint ink (guard LT5). ## UI Round 4 Phase 2C Plan 4 Admin, Asset and Login Forms and Dialogs (2026-10-14)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2c-plan4` (planned on `master` `c2bfbe2` and rebased before Task 1 onto `master` `a667f6f`: 2A, 2C plans 1–3 and 2B plans 1–4 merged, not deployed), built in the git worktree `.worktrees/ui-phase2c-plan4` after plan 2B-4 merged (the user chose one plan at a time, 2B-4 first). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-ui-phase2c-design.md` §8.9 batch 4 (its §10 rulings I1–I36 override the section files) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-13-ui-phase2c-plan4.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`.
  - Batch 4 is 18 files in `src/components/`: `admin/admin-user-form.tsx`, `admin/role-permission-form.tsx`, `admin/IntegrationClientManager.tsx`, `admin/asset-state-review-dialog.tsx`, `admin/data-quality-rule-form.tsx`, `notifications/notification-center-actions.tsx`, `reports/report-preset-controls.tsx`, `auth/login-form.tsx`, `assets/asset-label-batch-tool.tsx`, `assets/asset-label-print.tsx`, `assets/asset-scan-search-tool.tsx`, `assets/asset-status-correction-button.tsx`, `assets/asset-evidence-drawer.tsx`, `ui/activity-drawer.tsx`, `asset-operations/transaction-cancel-dialog.tsx`, `assets/asset-create-workspace.tsx`, the bulk update dialog of `assets/asset-register-table.tsx` and `assets/asset-import-preview-panel.tsx`. One commit per file (Tasks 1–18); a page that only feeds a component its labels changed in that component's commit (`D/admin/data-quality/page.tsx` in Task 5, `D/assets/page.tsx` and `D/asset-management/import-export/page.tsx` in Task 18).
  - No new primitive, workflow, page, schema, RBAC, migration, dependency or token. No server change. Validation is unchanged (K4): every `required`, `maxLength`, `minLength`, `pattern`, toast and `disabled` gate stays.

Plans 2C-4 and 2B-4 share no code file.

Two exceptions: the login form (`FieldLabel` + `Input`: its tests pin the literal `htmlFor`, and the password frame holds the show-password button) and the label-print toolbar (K21, below). Every checkbox uses `checkboxVariants`. A button that starts work has `busy` and keeps its label (K13: no "กำลังบันทึก...", "กำลังเข้าสู่ระบบ...", "กำลังนำเข้า..." or "..." swaps), so focus stays on it.

The three with a form wrap body and footer in `<form className={accessibleDialogFormClasses}>`; the cancel dialog (layout B) runs its confirm on click. None of them passes `closeLabel` any more, so every X reads "ปิด" (the integration dialog's X read "ยกเลิก").
  - `admin/admin-user-form.tsx`: 5 fields; the local `Field` helper is gone; cancel is an outline link and save is `busy`.

## UI Round 4 Phase 2B Plan 5 Admin, Reports, Dashboard, Notifications and the Guard Lock (2026-10-15)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2b-plan5` (from `master` `6835bdf`: 2A, 2C plans 1–4 and 2B plans 1–4 merged, not deployed), in the main checkout beside 2C plan 5 in `.worktrees/ui-phase2c-plan5`. Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-ui-phase2b-design.md` (its §10 rulings I1–I34 override the section files in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-12-ui-phase2b/`; this plan's files are §8.9) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-ui-phase2b-plan5.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`.
  - Plan 2B-5, the last plan of phase 2B, moves the 25 files of spec §8.9 onto the 2B-1 primitives: `/admin/roles`, `/admin/logs`, `/admin/storage`, `/admin/approvals`, `/admin/approvals/history`, `/admin/readiness`, the header of `/admin/settings`, `/access-denied`, `/notifications`, `/my-assets`, the reports page with its filter panel, view tabs, wide-list table and four views, the dashboard and the work center. It deletes `ReportHeader`, `ReportActiveFilters`, `MetricCard` and the old helpers of `master-data-layout.tsx`, and locks the guards. One commit per file; the history page's where clause in `src/lib/approval-decision-log.ts` is a commit of its own (with `tests/approval-decision-log.test.ts`), and each deleted file went in the commit of its last caller.
  - No new primitive, schema, migration, API, RBAC, dependency, index or token. Three lists get read-only queries for their pages (below); seven message keys are new.
- **Phase 2B is complete.** 2D may start once this branch and 2C plan 5 have both merged (spec B7, §8.1); the V track follows 2D (I31), then the one deploy of phase 2.
- **Deploy:** none, as for 2B plans 1–4: one deploy after all of phase 2; `ab1b873` stays the rollback point (no schema change).
- **Real pages for the last three lists (B4, spec §5.9):** each reads `page` and `pageSize` with `parseListPage` (25 by default; 25/50/100), takes `listPageWindow`, counts in the same `Promise.all` with the old `orderBy` plus `{ id: "asc" }`, and redirects a page past the end to the last page with the same filters and page size.
  - `/admin/logs` (it stopped at the newest 100): `systemLog.count({ where })`; the filters keep the page size. `buildSystemLogFilterHref` (`src/lib/system-log-history.ts`) is unchanged: the page adds the page size to the quick-filter links, so the default links read as before.
  - `/admin/approvals/history` (it stopped at the newest 150): the decisions a user may see moved into `buildApprovalDecisionLogWhere` (`src/lib/approval-decision-log.ts`; `tests/approval-decision-log.test.ts` proves it keeps exactly the rows of the old filter for every permission and tab); one window of rows, its `count` and a `groupBy` over every allowed decision (`summarizeApprovalDecisionCounts`), so the strip and the tab counts are full totals. The tabs keep the page size and start at page 1.
  - `/my-assets` (no pages): one window of rows, `asset.count` and a light read of every asset the person holds (the status and one photo id) for the four totals. The detail page's back link still lands on page 1 (2D, below).
  - The two admin lists run inside `withPerformanceTiming` (`admin-logs.list-data`, `approval-history.list-data`). Load time on the production build (median `responseEnd`, before → after): no production number before (the before images came from the dev server) → `/th/admin/logs` 217 ms and `/th/admin/approvals/history` 166 ms (median of five loads each; `/th/my-assets` 90 ms, the seed admin holds no asset); nothing felt slower than the other admin pages, and the count times are in `docs/03_DATABASE.md`.
- **What changed, file by file:**
  - `D/admin/roles/page.tsx`: `PageHeader` with the export as an outline `md` button; the three tinted boxes are one `StatStrip` (`attention` while an inactive role still has users); the role list and the permission matrix are `DataTable framed={false}` in `ContentPanel divided flush`, keys in `tag`, counts through `formatCount`, an empty matrix cell `Nil`; each role row has "แก้ไข" through `RowActions` (`next` only, `groups={[]}`: roles have no delete API), pinned right; the role list has an empty row (the matrix lists a fixed module list and cannot be empty).
