## UI Round 4 Phase 2C Plan 5 Detail-Page Action Bars, Confirm Dialogs, Disposal Steps and Settings Tabs (2026-10-15)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2c-plan5` (planned on `master` `6835bdf` and rebased before Task 1 onto `master` `cbb98a9`: 2A, 2C plans 1–4 and 2B plans 1–5 merged, not deployed), built in the git worktree `.worktrees/ui-phase2c-plan5` after plan 2B-5 merged (the user chose one plan at a time, 2B-5 first). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-ui-phase2c-design.md` §8.9 batch 5 (its §10 rulings I1–I36 override the section files) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-ui-phase2c-plan5.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`. Batch 5 is the last of 2C.
  - Batch 5 crosses domains: one `ActionBar` (`ui/action-bar.tsx`, new) for the phone bars of `D/assets/[id]/page.tsx`, `D/maintenance/[id]/page.tsx`, `D/disposal/[id]/page.tsx` and `D/audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx` (O11; `ui/mobile-action-bar.tsx` and `disposal/disposal-mobile-action-bar.tsx` are deleted); `disposal/disposal-workflow-stepper.tsx` on `Steps` (S1); `ui/alert-dialog.tsx` with the dialog look (K12; `ui/confirm-dialog.tsx` needed no change); `maintenance/maintenance-attachments.tsx` on `useConfirm`; a verb on the 4 confirms that still said "ยืนยัน" (`admin/IntegrationClientManager.tsx`, `disposal/disposal-bulk-approval.tsx`, `disposal/disposal-bulk-execution.tsx`, `master-data/supplier-form.tsx`); and the settings tab row of `admin/system-settings-form.tsx` on `TabNav` (O7).
  - Commits: `ActionBar` alone (Task 1); the swap in one commit, the two old bars deleted together with the four pages' bars, root padding and the tests that read them (Task 2); then one per file (Tasks 3–9; `alert-dialog.tsx` and `confirm-dialog.tsx` apart in Task 4, the two bulk confirms apart in Task 7), each with the page line and message keys its own type needs (`D/disposal/[id]/page.tsx` in Task 3, `D/admin/integrations/page.tsx` in Task 6, one line of `D/disposal/page.tsx` in each part of Task 7); and the guard comments (Task 10).
  - No new workflow, page, schema, RBAC, migration, API, dependency, index or token. No server change. Validation is unchanged (K4): every `required`, `maxLength`, `minLength`, toast and `disabled` gate stays.
- **2D may start once this branch merges** (2B spec B7, §8.1; plan 2B-5 merged first, `cbb98a9`): with both plans 5 merged, 2B and 2C are done, and 2D (its own spec) widens the guards to all of `src`. Plans 2C-5 and 2B-5 share no code file (2B spec §8.3).
- **Deploy:** none: one deploy after all of phase 2 and the user's checks on real devices; `ab1b873` stays the rollback point (no schema change).
- **What changed in each file:**
  - `c/ui/action-bar.tsx` (new; K23, spec §3.15, §6.10, I23): server-safe (no directive and no hook), so the four server pages render it. A `role="group"` named by its required `label` (the pages pass `tCommon("actions")`, "การดำเนินการ"), fixed to the bottom below `md` (`md:hidden`) on `bg-surface` with a `border-t` and the home-bar padding; its row is centered and at most 512px wide on every page (only the disposal bar had that). Up to 4 links in one row, or 3 under a full-width `primaryAction`. Each link is `buttonVariants({ size: "sm" })` with `min-w-0 px-2`: 44px with 13/20 text, `outline`, or `default` when the link sets `primary` (the old bars were grey-filled with `text-xs leading-tight`). A disabled item is `<a role="link" aria-disabled="true">` with no `href`, in the 2A disabled look (it was a plain `<span>`). `actionBarPagePadding.single` (`pb-[calc(6rem+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] md:pb-0`) and `.withPrimary` (9rem) add the home bar to the old 96px and 144px (spec §6.15 rulings 15–16).
  - The four O11 pages changed their bar import, root padding and bar only: `` `space-y-6 ${actionBarPagePadding.single}` `` on the asset and repair pages, `` `space-y-6 ${actionBarPagePadding.withPrimary}` `` on the disposal page and `actionBarPagePadding.single` alone on the audit round page (its root had no `space-y-6`); every bar passes `label={tCommon("actions")}`, and the disposal page passes `primary: !nextActionControl` on its first link (the old bar's rule moved to the page). Back items (2B spec §4.6.4, R3): the repair page's bar dropped "กลับ" and `ArrowLeft` left its imports, because its header has `PageBackLink` at every width since 2B plan 4 (the bar keeps open asset, print and history); the audit round page keeps its back item `D/audit/rounds/[id]/page.tsx:496`, because its header has no back button.
  - `c/disposal/disposal-workflow-stepper.tsx` (S1, K17): the file and `DisposalWorkflowStepper` stay as an adapter, and the hand-drawn `<ol>` is `Steps` with `getLinearStepStates(3, …)`. A request that can be opened has been filed: a pending one is current at "พิจารณา" (it showed the first step as current), an approved one at "ตัดจำหน่ายจริง", a rejected one stops at "พิจารณา" with the X, and a disposed one has every step done and none current. The steps are named for what they do, "ยื่นคำขอ · พิจารณา · ตัดจำหน่ายจริง" (they were the card titles "รายละเอียด…"); the list is named "ขั้นตอนการตัดจำหน่าย" (new prop `stepsLabel`), and phones show one line such as "ขั้นที่ 2 จาก 3 · พิจารณา". The panel and its stage row did not change (K28, 2D).
  - `c/ui/alert-dialog.tsx` (K12, spec §5.9): the confirm dialog has the dialog look: a white box with 12px corners and `shadow-overlay`, 12px above the bottom edge and the home bar on phones and centered up to 512px from `sm`, a column that clips at `92dvh`; the header (the 16px semibold title, left-aligned, then the 14px description) carries the bottom line; the footer is the `background` band with Cancel first, stacked full width on phones and a right-aligned row from `sm`. The content's `size` prop, `data-size` and `AlertDialogMedia` are gone (no caller). The box and its phone position are two class strings, as in `dialog.tsx` (R5), so guard GL1 does not count the dialog as a fixed bottom bar.
  - `c/ui/confirm-dialog.tsx`: no source change (R6). It already put Cancel first and passes the caller's `confirmLabel` (falling back to "ยืนยัน") and tone; a new test pins that, and the file joined `MIGRATED_FILES`.
  - `c/maintenance/maintenance-attachments.tsx`: deleting an attachment asks through `useConfirm` ("ต้องการลบรายการนี้หรือไม่?", the red "ลบ", `tone: "destructive"`) at the top of `handleDelete`, as the other attachment lists do (R8); the home-made dialog, its `pendingDeleteId` state and the `AccessibleDialog` import are gone, and the toast after a delete says "ลบแล้ว" (`common.deletedSuccess`; it said "บันทึกสำเร็จ"). The cards and their buttons, the delete button's spinner included, did not change (2D).
  - The four confirms that said "ยืนยัน" name their action (K12, spec §5.9–5.10; `title` stays the first property): the scope expansion of `c/admin/IntegrationClientManager.tsx` says "เพิ่มสิทธิ์" (default tone; the page passes `confirmScopeExpansionAction`), the discard-selection confirms of `c/disposal/disposal-bulk-approval.tsx` and `c/disposal/disposal-bulk-execution.tsx` say "ละทิ้งรายการที่เลือก" (destructive; one key for both copies, set in `D/disposal/page.tsx`), and the unsaved-changes confirm of `c/master-data/supplier-form.tsx` says "ออกโดยไม่บันทึก" (destructive). What each button does is unchanged.
  - `c/admin/system-settings-form.tsx` (O7, 2B spec R-g): the 11 tabs are `TabNav`, an underline row without the old card. Each tab is a link to its own `?tab=` URL, and `onSelect` calls `event.preventDefault()` and `selectTab(…)`, so a click switches in place through `replaceState` and unsaved edits stay, while Ctrl+click opens the tab's URL. ## UI Round 4 Phase 2D Plan 2 Admin, Reports, Dashboard and Shell (2026-10-16)

- Branch `feat/ui-phase2d-plan2` in the main checkout (from `master` `da8388d`: phase 1, 2A, 2B plans 1–5, 2C plans 1–5 and 2D plan 1 merged, not deployed). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-15-ui-phase2d-design.md` §3.2 · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-15-ui-phase2d-plan2.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md`. The second of five 2D plans, one at a time (D15).
- No new workflow, page, schema, migration, dependency, token or API change. Validation is unchanged. One visible permission change the spec asked for (D11): the users page shows its create button only with `user:create` (the page `/admin/users/new` already refused others).
- **Shared parts (Task 1):** the close X of `DialogContent` (`c/ui/dialog.tsx`) and `SheetContent` (`c/ui/sheet.tsx`) comes before the content, so the Tab order is X → content → footer and Shift+Tab from the first field reaches the X (D18). `focusFirstContentControl` (`c/ui/dialog-focus.ts`, Radix's own first-focus rule minus the X) runs from `onOpenAutoFocus` after the caller's handler, so focus still opens on the first control; `c/ui/accessible-dialog.tsx` did not change (R1). New `Chip` (`c/ui/chip.tsx`): the neutral 22px name chip (R2). `splitStatRows` moved to `src/lib/stat-strip-rows.ts` (R7).
- **Shell (Task 2, R10, R11):** the topbar menu, sidebar toggle and bell are ghost icon `Button`s (36px from `md`, were 44px at every width), the language and user triggers ghost `md`, the scan link outline `md`; the notification list shows its counts as `StatusBadge`s and "nothing new" as `EmptyNote`. The right group is `shrink-0` and the search width moved to its anchor in `c/layout/global-search.tsx` (two classes; the rest of that file is phase 3), so at 1024–1180px CSS width the search narrows instead of running under the bell (D18). The sidebar drawer's close is a ghost icon `Button`.
- **Admin (Tasks 3–14):** the settings form on 16 `ContentPanel`s, `Notice` by meaning (R5), three `DataTable`s, `KeyValueList`s for the overview and the document examples, two `StatStrip`s in the LDAP sync preview, keys, prefixes and tokens as `tag` (R3), the change-review key link switching tabs in place (`Link onNavigate`), the label preview on tokens, and the presets `md:min-h-9` (2D-1 R3). The depreciation builder lost its own frame (R13). The integration client manager (M27, R12): `StatStrip`, the token panel with a warning `Notice`, `DataTable`, the shared `StatusBadge`, scope `Chip`s, default `Button`s in the row with rotate `busy`. The role permission form; roles and users with create in `PageHeader` (M13, R8), `Chip`s and `StatusBadge`; the admin user form; storage (archive `Button busy`, M14); data quality (the review queue with an `md` scan button, rule cards with a severity `StatusBadge`, the rule form); readiness, approvals and history (R6 tones); the system log; access denied (`md`, 2D-1 R3).

## Audit Counting on Phones and the Findings Table (2026-10-16)

- Branch `feat/audit-mobile-fix` in the worktree `.worktrees/audit-mobile-fix` (from `master` `2fcfad2`, rebased onto `master` `bed4f16` — the Noto Sans Thai Looped font change — before merging; the app check was repeated on the rebased build with the same numbers: phase 1, 2A, 2B plans 1–5, 2C plans 1–5 and 2D plans 1–2 merged, not deployed). A bounded fix the user approved on 2026-10-16 before plan 2D-3, with decisions D1–D8 (brief `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-16-audit-mobile-fix/brief.md`, git-ignored). Commits `7a88ac7` (sheet), `f0523d8` (components), `3c51e40` (scan page), `2358c61`, `0516ba9`, `55ef773` and `3e11101` (findings table).
- No new workflow, page, schema, permission, migration, dependency, token or API change. Validation, the offline queue, the photo queue and the save payload are unchanged.
- **Why (measured on the dev app):** field staff count by typed search (QR labels are not printed yet). On a phone with browser bars (390 × 664 CSS px) the check sheet's content was 688px in a 525px scroll area, so "บันทึก · ตรงทุกข้อ" sat below the fold for every asset; one component card was 275px (three stacked buttons) and an asset with one component made the sheet 1,121px; the scan page header took about 300px before the first row, a round with no room chosen listed all 45 items, row rooms were truncated and search results had no room. On desktop the findings table had 13 columns, 2,272px wide in a 965px box (1280 screen) or 1,125px (1440), with approve and reject in the last column, so reviewers scrolled right for every row.
- **Check sheet (D1, `c/audit/audit-scan-check-form.tsx`, `audit-scan-check-panel.tsx`, `audit-scan-check-field.tsx`):** the form is a flex column with a scrolling body (`data-audit-check-body`: status line, fields, installed-in notice, components, "หมายเหตุ / รูป") and an action bar outside the scroll area (`data-audit-check-actions`, `shrink-0 border-t`: the missing-photo reason, the components hint, save and "ไม่ใช่ชิ้นนี้" side by side, then the approver's immediate-correction checkbox). The phone sheet hands its height to the form (no scrolling wrapper); the ≥1024px side panel is `max-h-[calc(100dvh-7rem)]` with its title in a header block, so the bar sits at the panel's bottom. A matching field has no box (label above the control); a mismatch keeps the warning-soft box and "ในระบบ: …". The save label, variant, busy and disabled logic did not change; the immediate-correction checkbox now comes after "ไม่ใช่ชิ้นนี้" in the Tab order.
- **Components (D2–D4, `c/audit/audit-scan-panels.tsx` `AuditComponentPanel`, the form, `audit-scan-workspace.tsx`, `audit-scan-helpers.ts`):** the list shows at once (no "ชิ้นส่วนย่อย (n)" toggle; a `role="status"` loading line while it loads), headed "ส่วนควบ {n} ชิ้น" with "ตรวจแล้ว {checked} / {n}". Each component is one row (`min-h-16`): tag and `StatusBadge`, then name · role · slot on one truncated line, an outline "พบ" `Button` (a check mark once confirmed) and a ⋯ `DropdownMenu` with "สแกน QR ส่วนควบ" and "ไม่พบส่วนควบนี้" (opens the existing missing dialog). `dropdown-menu` was used rather than `RowActions`, which has no disabled state and would open a second sheet on a phone. Out-of-round components keep their disabled "พบ"/"ไม่พบ" and badge. "พบครบทุกชิ้น" in the heading confirms each pending component in turn through the same `confirmComponent` (now `Promise<boolean>`), stops on the first failure and is `busy` while it runs; a reload of the same asset keeps the rows. Saving the parent while components are pending asks first (`useConfirm`, "ส่วนควบยังตรวจไม่ครบ {count} ชิ้น", confirm "บันทึกตัวหลัก"); a hint "ส่วนควบตรวจแล้ว {checked} / {total}" sits above save while some are pending. New helper `isAuditComponentPending`.
- **Scan page (D5–D7, `c/audit/audit-scan-header.tsx`, `audit-scan-room-picker.tsx`, `audit-scan-room-list.tsx`, `audit-scan-search.tsx`, `use-audit-scan-room.ts`, the workspace, `D/audit/rounds/[id]/scan/page.tsx` one line `roundCode`):** the header is one line (back link, the h1 showing the round code on phones and "code - name" from `md`, screen readers always get the full name; "ตรวจแล้ว x / y · ไม่ตรง n" on the right) over a 4px progress bar. The room button shows the room, "ยังไม่ตรวจ a / b" and "เปลี่ยน". Rows are tag / name / room in full (wrapping, no ellipsis; left out when a room is chosen), and each search result has a room line, "อยู่ <room>" in warning ink when it is in another room. The picker is controlled and opens by itself once per visit for a round with more than one room and no stored choice (`hasStoredRoom` from the hook; "ไม่เลือกห้อง" is stored too; X or Esc dismisses it for the visit; a deep link to an asset does not open it).
- **Findings table (D8, `D/audit/findings/page.tsx`, `c/audit/audit-finding-review-actions.tsx`; desktop only, the phone cards are unchanged):** 8 columns in a fixed layout with a `<colgroup>` (`minWidth={960}`): วันที่รายงาน · รอบตรวจ · ทรัพย์สิน · ประเภท · "ในระบบ → พบ" (`FindingValueChange`: system value, arrow, found value in medium weight, no box; evidence on one line) · สถานะ (`FindingStatusBadges`: the five statuses as `StatusBadge`s, only those that carry information, each with a tooltip and an sr-only prefix) · ผู้รับผิดชอบ (owner, then "กำหนด {date}"; `Nil` when empty) · การดำเนินการ (`sticky="right"`). `AuditFindingReviewActions compact` keeps the review decision in the cell (approve and reject stacked, or the self-review note without a box) and puts "แผนแก้ไข", "ปิดรายการไม่ตรง" and the disposal-request link in a `RowActions` ⋯; each item sets the existing dialog state, and `ActionPlanModal` and `CloseFindingModal` return focus to ⋯ (`returnFocusRef`). Without review rights the cell is a ⋯ with only the disposal link, or `Nil`.
- **The user's decisions (D1–D8):** pinned sheet buttons and compact matching fields; components expanded as one row each; "พบครบทุกชิ้น"; ask before saving the parent with components pending; one-line scan header; full room names in rows and search; the room picker opens by itself once; the findings table in 8 columns with the actions pinned right.
- **Ruling (findings row ⋯):** after the app showed 180–288px rows (three stacked secondary buttons), the row's secondary actions went into ⋯ and approve/reject stayed visible, following the 2B row-action decision ช1 (the mockup showed only approve and reject). It reverses 2B's "the findings review triggers stay in the cell" for plan and close. Cost if wrong: one more tap for plan or close; revertible.
- **Messages (through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`):** `auditScan` added `componentFound`, `componentMarkMissing`, `componentsConfirmAll`, `componentsLoading`, `componentsSaveHint`, `componentsPendingSaveTitle`, `componentsPendingSaveDescription` and `componentsPendingSaveAction`; changed `componentsSection` ("ชิ้นส่วนย่อย ({count})" → "ส่วนควบ {count} ชิ้น") and `componentsCheckedCount` ("ตรวจแล้ว {checked}/{total}" → "ตรวจแล้ว {checked} / {total}"); deleted `componentsPanelTitle`, `componentsPanelHelp`, `componentConfirmWithParent` and `componentMissing`. `auditFinding` added `comparisonColumn` ("ในระบบ → พบ") and `dueOn` ("กำหนด {date}"); `ownerDueColumn` was added and deleted again within the branch. `auditFinding.comparison` is no longer read by the page and stays (a test checks it).
- **Tests:** no new file; `tests/audit-scan-check-ui.test.ts` +3, `tests/audit-component-scan-ui.test.ts` +5 (2 pins rewritten: `onConfirmComponent={confirmComponent}` and the copy keys), `tests/audit-scan-room-ui.test.ts` +5, `tests/audit-scan-search-ui.test.ts` +1; `tests/list-pages-ui.test.ts` replaced "the findings actions cell keeps its buttons on one line" with the D8 test (colSpan 8, colgroup, sticky head, ⋯ items, `returnFocusRef`); `tests/audit-item-status-labels.test.ts` (labels as badge `label:`) and `tests/audit-finding-resolution.test.ts` (the two new keys) edited. 2051 → 2065 tests.
- **Guards:** no row changed and no number went up; `tests/form-migration-guards.test.ts` changed only the comment of the form's `LOADER2` row (still 1: the toggle's spinner is now the loading line's).
- Verification: `npm run verify` passes (tests 2,065 · pass 2,064 · skipped 1 · build 60/60 pages).
- Dev-app check: checked on `next start` (port 3101) of the production build of the worktree at `3e11101` with the dev database `asset_management_dev`, as the seed admin (Playwright); verify there: lint 0 errors (146 warnings, all under `.agents/`), tests 2065 / 2064 pass / 0 fail / 1 skipped, build 60/60 pages. Nothing was written: 0 POST, PUT, PATCH or DELETE in every run (the read-only `scan-lookup` POST excluded); no save, confirm, "พบ", "พบครบทุกชิ้น", plan or close was submitted. Before → after at 390 × 664: the check sheet of an asset without components went from a 688px body in a 525px area (save below the fold) to 432 / 432 (no scroll), save at 604–652 of 664; an asset with 1 component went from 1,121px of content and a 275px card to a 561px body in 422 (139px scroll), a 64px component row and save always visible at 604–652, with "ส่วนควบ 1 ชิ้น", "พบครบทุกชิ้น" and the hint "ส่วนควบตรวจแล้ว 0 / 1". Saving with the component pending opens "ส่วนควบยังตรวจไม่ครบ 1 ชิ้น" (ยกเลิก / บันทึกตัวหลัก); Esc keeps the sheet open and sends nothing. The component ⋯ holds "สแกน QR ส่วนควบ" and "ไม่พบส่วนควบนี้". Scan page: the room picker opened by itself on the first visit with "ยังไม่ตรวจ a / b" per room; after choosing ACC_FL4 the list shows 7 items and the room button reads "ACC_FL4 - ACC_FL4 · ยังไม่ตรวจ 5 / 7 · เปลี่ยน"; a reload does not open it again; the header is one line, code + "ตรวจแล้ว 12 / 45 · ไม่ตรง 1". Search "dell": each result shows "อยู่ <room>". At 1440 the side panel keeps its action bar at the bottom (save bottom 785 of 900). Findings table: 13 columns at 2,272px in a 965 / 1,125px box → 8 columns, scrollWidth = box at 1280 (965) and 1440 (1,125), no horizontal scroll; 1024 scrolls with the action column pinned; the header is one 40px line; rows 114–156px at 1280 (208–288 after the first pass); approve and reject show when allowed, otherwise the compact self-review note; "แผนแก้ไข / ปิดรายการไม่ตรง / เปิดคำขอตัดจำหน่าย" are in ⋯ (the plan dialog opens, and Esc returns focus to ⋯); the phone cards are unchanged (no table at 390, no page scroll). axe (color-contrast, label, aria-valid-attr-value, aria-required-attr, button-name, link-name, nested-interactive, target-size, list, listitem): 0 on the findings page (1280, 390), the scan page (1440) and the open check sheet (390). ## Dark Mode (2026-10-17)

- Branch `feat/dark-mode` in the main checkout (from `master` `c0ad2aa`: phase 1, 2A, 2B, 2C and 2D merged, not deployed, plus the spec's commits `9957e75` and `87e4756`). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-dark-mode-design.md` (the user's decisions U1–U5) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-dark-mode.md` · canonical values in `DESIGN.md` (§2 Dark Theme and The Two-Theme Rule).
- No migration, database write, schema, API or dependency change (no `next-themes`). Validation is unchanged. Messages (through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`): 5 keys added, `common.switchToDark`, `common.switchToLight`, `common.pageNotFoundTitle`, `common.pageNotFoundDescription` and `common.backToHome`: 4062 → 4067 keys.
- **Behaviour:** the page follows the OS until the person chooses. The toggle beside the language button switches light ⇄ dark at once (a moon while light, a sun while dark, right from the first paint), and the `theme` cookie (`theme=<choice>; Path=/; Max-Age=31536000; SameSite=Lax`) keeps the choice in that browser, also after logging out. The root layout renders `data-theme` on `<html>` from the cookie (none: follow the OS) and `theme-color` (one color for a choice, the light/dark `media` pair without one), so the first paint is right with no script. The login page has no toggle and follows the cookie or the OS.
- **Tokens (Tasks 1–5):** the dark set, direction A "navy dark", in `:root[data-theme="dark"]` and its identical copy under `@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root:not([data-theme="light"]) }`; `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` runs every rule on both sets (20 → 36 tests). The scrim, the overlay shadow, the field halos and the select chevron are variables the dark blocks re-declare (`--elevation-overlay`, `--halo-field-*`, `--select-chevron-image`; T-R4, T-R5). One forced-light scope `[data-theme="light"]` and one print reset carry the light set and effects (T-R6, T-R7); `color-scheme` is `light dark`, `dark` or `only light` and `accent-color` is `--primary` (T-R8); every ring gap is `--card` (T-R9). New tokens: `--image-stage` (#E9EEF5 in both themes) and `--app-icon-frame` (#FFFFFF light, #34496A dark).
- **Paper and imagery (Tasks 6–11):** `data-theme="light"` on exactly four paper surfaces: the A4 document page (its VOID banner inside), the label sheet, the label sample in Admin › Settings and the signature canvas (P-R2–P-R5). `bg-image-stage` on the 15 uploaded images (P-R1); the bell's count dot takes `text-danger-foreground` and its N1 row went (P-R7); the app icon gets a 1px frame in the sidebar and on login (P-R6). QR tiles, the camera, the preview stage and the server PDF are unchanged (P-R8).
- **Shell (Tasks 12–19):** `src/lib/theme.ts` (the cookie, its parsing, `THEME_COLORS` = `--sidebar` of each theme); `readThemeChoice()` and `generateViewport` in `src/app/layout.tsx`; `ThemeToggle` (`src/components/layout/theme-toggle.tsx`) with faces chosen by CSS (`data-when-theme`); the topbar and the phone bottom bar on `bg-sidebar`; the login headings on `text-foreground`; the app's own 404 `src/app/not-found.tsx`; `public/offline.html` follows the OS and `public/sw.js` caches `asset-system-pwa-v3`.
- **Light theme:** unchanged except A-R11: the bars' fill token (the same white), the login headings `#083161` → `#0B1D35`, the app's own 404 page, the asset page's gallery photo buttons `#F6F8FB` → `#E9EEF5` (the image stage), and `color-scheme: light dark` on a root with no choice (a dark OS without a cookie now gets dark, the spec's default U2).
- **Guards and pins:** `dark:` and `@custom-variant dark` stay banned. The pins that encoded light only were rewritten in the commits that changed what they pin: VFG (the base and `color-scheme` pins, the ring gap, the `<html>` string, the bottom bar, the offline page and the cache name), `tests/modern-enterprise-theme.test.ts`, `tests/ui-overlay-guards.test.ts`, `tests/html-lang-and-years.test.ts`, `tests/visual-consistency-ui.test.ts`, `tests/dashboard-shell-theme.test.ts` and `tests/login-page-ui.test.ts`. N1 lost the topbar row (8 → 7 rows); no `EXEMPT` row or owner was added.
- **Docs:** `DESIGN.md` (The Two-Theme Rule in place of the Light Only Rule, §2 Dark Theme with every dark value, the navy-sidebar Don't scoped to the light theme, surfaces named by role, the toggle and `theme-color` in §5 Navigation), `docs/14_UI_UX_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (the two-theme reminder, the image stage and paper rows, the QA line, the Themes registry row) and `.impeccable/design.json` (the dark value of each color and shadow, The Two-Theme Rule). `tests/dark-mode-docs.test.ts` keeps "Light Only" out of the three and checks their dark values against `globals.css`. The "light only" lines of the dated sections above are history; this section closes them. The 2D-5 changelog pin now finds its entry under 2026-10-17 (D-R5).
- **Tests:** new files `tests/dark-mode-t.test.ts` (4), `tests/dark-mode-p.test.ts` (7), `tests/dark-mode-s.test.ts` (8) and `tests/dark-mode-docs.test.ts` (3); `tests/design-tokens-contrast.test.ts` 20 → 36; 2188 → 2226 tests.
- **Rulings of this plan:** R1 (the earlier rulings), R2 (the dark set and the theme rules, T-R1–T-R12), R3 (paper and imagery, P-R1–P-R9), R4 (the shell, S-R1–S-R9), R5 (assembly, A-R1–A-R11), R6 (the docs, D-R1–D-R6).
- **The user's answers to the open issues (2026-10-17):** T-a, dialogs and sheets keep the panel color in dark (T-R11; re-judged from the Task 22 screenshots: keep); P-a, the image stage stays `#E9EEF5` (P-R1); S-a, the 404 page's words as written (S-R7); S-b, the toggle without a tooltip (S-R4).
- **Not taken:** `next-themes`, an inline theme script, a `dark:` variant, a second app icon for dark, a dark manifest splash, `global-not-found` and `global-error` (S-R7), a dimmer dark image stage (P-R1), `bg-popover` for dark dialogs and sheets (T-R11).
- **Handed on:** the V track (errors under fields, I31); then the E2E suite, which runs every workflow in both themes (spec §7); then the one deploy (wiki 27).
- Verification: `npm run verify` passes (tests 2,226 · pass 2,225 · skipped 1 · build 60/60 pages).
- Dev-app check: checked (Task 22) on `next start` (port 3110) of the production build of the branch at `8e22a38` with the dev database, as the seed admin; verify there: lint 0 errors (146 warnings, all under `.agents/`), tests 2225 / 2224 pass / 0 fail / 1 skipped, tsc clean, build 60/60 pages, every app route dynamic (only `apple-icon`, `icon` and the manifest static) and `/_not-found` dynamic (the app's own page). Nothing was written: 0 POST, PUT, PATCH or DELETE on every run. Counts after: 4 literal `data-theme="light"` in components (the A4 page, the label sheet, the settings label sample, the signature canvas); 19 images, 15 with `bg-image-stage`; `text-brand-navy` 0; `bg-sidebar` on the topbar and the bottom bar; theme code only in `globals.css`, `layout.tsx`, `theme-toggle.tsx` and `theme.ts`; N1 `RAW_COLOR_EXEMPT` 7 rows; 4067 Thai keys. The four states on `/th/dashboard` at 1440: a dark choice gives `data-theme="dark"`, `color-scheme` dark, one `theme-color` #0D182A, topbar and sidebar rgb(13,24,42) and the sun; a light choice `only light`, #FFFFFF, white bars and the moon; no cookie with a light OS `light dark`, the `media` pair, white bars and the moon; no cookie with a dark OS `light dark`, the `media` pair, rgb(13,24,42) and the sun. 12 views (dashboard, register, asset page, new asset, rounds, scan page, disposal, maintenance, reports, settings, brands, 404) in dark and light at 1440, 768 and 375 (72 shots in `after/`): axe color-contrast 0 violations in all 48 runs (1440 and 375, both themes), no sideways scroll at 375 in any view. Toggle: 36×36 at 1440 and 44×44 at 375, beside TH; a press switches at once, writes `theme=light` / `theme=dark` and `theme-color` follows; a reload keeps the choice; an OS change after a choice changes nothing; the login page has no toggle and follows the cookie (dark headings rgb(228,235,245)). No flash: a throttled load (400ms latency, 200kB/s, CPU 4×) of `/th/dashboard` in all four states, the body color sampled every 100ms, showed only the final theme's color from the first paint (dark rgb(13,25,44), light rgb(246,248,251)). Paper and print: in dark the A4 maintenance record is white paper with dark ink on a dark page (the scope `only light`); the label sheet and the settings label sample stay white; the signature canvas is white inside its scope; print preview from a dark page is light (white body, `only light`); the A4 print preview from dark is pixel-identical to Task 0's light one (0.00% differ), and the dashboard prints light from dark. Light against Task 0 (pixel diff): 0.32% at 1440 and 0.78% at 375 on every view, which is the new toggle button in the topbar, and nothing else (the register's 31% is a saved search in Task 0's dev session, not a theme change). Dialogs in dark (T-a): the select popover and the filter sheet read well, the sheet on the panel color over the scrim (`after/filter-sheet-dark-375.png`, `after/dialog-dark-1440.png`, `after/menu-dark-1440.png`). The 404 page, signed in and out, in dark and light, follows the choice (signed-out dark rgb(13,25,44)); the offline page follows the OS. Forced colors (emulated) with a dark choice: system colors win, buttons and fields outlined, focus visible. Accepted mixes: white paper on a dark page (A4 documents, label sheet, settings label sample, signature canvas), white QR tiles, the camera controls' own dark fill, the attachment preview stage black/white, uploaded images on the light #E9EEF5 stage. Found, not caused by this plan: the first verify build served the old light-only CSS from the Turbopack build cache (no dark tokens, no image stage, though the JS was new); moving `.next/cache/turbopack` away and building again fixed it (see Still open); attachment thumbnails return 500 on the dev machine (as before, wiki 25). No fixes (no code change).

## V Track Plan 1 (2026-10-18)

- Branch `feat/v-track` in the main checkout (from `master` `9e17cab`: phase 2D and Dark Mode merged, not deployed, plus the spec's commit `71e5251`). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-v-track-field-errors-design.md` (the user's decisions U1–U9) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-17-v-track-plan1.md` (plan 1 of the track: the server, the shared foundation, and the master-data and admin forms).
- No migration, database write, schema or dependency change (`react-hook-form` stays unused). No server rule was added (spec §6.3): every refusal existed, only its body gained `fieldErrors`. Messages (through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`): +29 keys (`apiErrors.recipientRequired`, seven duplicate keys `apiErrors.companyCodeExists` … `categoryCodeExists`, `apiErrors.customFieldNameDuplicate`, `apiErrors.integrationClientIdExists`, the 17 `common.validation.*`, `branch.saveNeedsCompany`, `location.saveNeedsBranch`) and −4 (`apiErrors.invalidSupplierData`, `supplier.duplicateCode`, `supplier.invalidEmail`, `supplier.fieldTooLong`): 4067 → 4092 keys.
- **The 400 (Tasks 1–2):** every route's `ZodError` answers `{ error: "Invalid request data", code: "VALIDATION_FAILED", fieldErrors }` (`validationFailedBody(error, keyFor?)` in `src/lib/api-error-exposure.ts`, through `errorResponse` and the transaction-cancellation helper). `fieldErrors` holds one code per field under its dotted zod path (`customFieldDefs.0.fieldName`), `_form` for an issue with no path; the first issue of a field wins. Codes (`src/lib/field-errors.ts`, `issueToFieldErrorCode`): a message the api-error catalog knows becomes its catalog key (`endBeforeStart`, `handoverModeRequired`, `licenseSeatsExceeded` …); otherwise `required`, `tooShort`, `tooLong`, `outOfRange`, `invalidDate`, `invalidNumber`, `invalidEmail`, `invalidFormat`, `invalidChoice` or `invalid` (S-R2's table). The module has no `@/` import, so tests and client code import it.
- **Special keys (Tasks 4–6):** the settings route keys an issue by the submitted setting (`settings.asset_label_12_width_mm`, `settingFieldKeyFor`); the asset batch by row or common part (`rows.<i>.<field>`, `common.<field>`; the per-row parse is re-keyed, `assetBatchItemIssuePath`), and its duplicate serials and tags put `duplicate` under every row holding one; checkout's `HANDOVER_*` and disposal execution's evidence branch keep their `code` and add `fieldErrors`.
- **Field refusals and duplicates (Tasks 2, 7–10, 29a):** `FieldRefusalError(message, fieldErrors, status = 400)` (thrown by a helper, answered by `errorResponse`), `fieldErrorResponse({ status, error, fieldErrors, code? })` and `uniqueConflictResponse(field, error)` (409, `code: "DUPLICATE"`) in `src/lib/field-error-response.ts`, which re-exports `isPrismaUniqueConstraintError`. Duplicates that ended in a 500 answer 409 under their field on create and update: company, department, employee and location `code`, brand and model `name` (from the unique index, `P2002`), and category `code` (checked before writing). The §6.2 refusals name their field: username, password, role name (409), permissions, branch code, manager, parent, serial number, purchase document number, checkout condition, status correction status, PM vendor, assignee; the import file rules go under `_form`. The last duplicates that ended in a 500 answer under their field too (Task 29a): a role renamed to another role's name (409 under `name`), a branch code an inactive branch of the company holds (the check reads every branch), two custom fields with one name in a category (409 `DUPLICATE` under each row's `customFieldDefs.<i>.fieldName`, `src/lib/category-custom-field-names.ts`) and an existing integration client id (409 under `clientId`); "Employee already has a user account" goes under `employeeId` (`employeeHasUser`, 400). Supplier is on the shared body and codes (`src/lib/supplier-api-error.ts`, `src/lib/supplier-form-errors.ts` and their tests are gone). The recipient rule of a disposal has its catalog key `recipientRequired`, and `tests/api-error-catalog.test.ts` reads `message:` literals too. Integration v1 (`src/app/api/integrations/v1/**`, `integrationErrorResponse`) is unchanged.
- **The client (Tasks 11–17):** the rule helpers `src/lib/validation-rules.ts` (`required`, `minLength`, `maxLength`, `range`, `email`, `pattern`, `notBefore`, `atLeastOne`, each a code or `null`); `common.validation.*` and the resolver `src/lib/field-error-client.ts` (`common.validation` first, then `apiErrors`, then `common.validation.invalid`); `src/lib/field-focus.ts` (focus the first wrong field and scroll `main` so it shows whole, 24px clear of the topbar and the sticky save bar); the hook `useFieldErrors` / `useValidationMessage` (`src/components/ui/use-field-errors.ts`: `setErrors`, `applyServer`, `clear`, `message`, `focusFirst`); `FieldError` without `role="alert"` (one announcement per submit: focus reads the field with its error) and `Field liveError` (a polite live region for errors shown while typing; the asset form's duplicate tag and serial); the SearchableSelect error look (`data-invalid` and `triggerInvalidClasses`, teal again while focused or open; `MaintenanceOptionSelect` and `AuditScanCheckField` pass `id` and `error` on); `FieldGroup` (`src/components/ui/field-group.tsx`).
- **The forms (Tasks 19–29):** brand, branch, category (its custom field and photo rows too), company, department, employee, location, asset model, supplier, the admin user, the role, the integration client create and edit, the asset state review dialog, the data-quality rules and the report preset name: `noValidate` straight after `onSubmit`, a module-level `validate()` that restates every constraint the controls carry, errors under the field (a group: one error), focus on the first wrong field, the server's `fieldErrors` on the same fields, no validation toast (a refusal that names no field keeps today's message). Content gates became errors on submit (the review reason's 10 characters, at least one integration scope); the branch and location saves without a company or a branch stay gated, `aria-disabled` with the reason beside the button. The integration create and edit, and the data-quality rules, show the server's reason instead of a fixed text. The data-quality rules and the report preset name became `<form>`s.
- **Guards and pins:** `tests/validation-guards.test.ts` (`V_SCOPE` 51 forms in 44 files, `NOT_IN_SCOPE` 2, `V_FORMS` 15 files, `SELECT_STAR_ONLY` 14 required selects in 10 files that count down; rules V1–V5; reader `tests/helpers/validation-reader.ts`). FMG's `STAR_ONLY` (3) and `CONSTRAINT_FLOORS` did not change; no `EXEMPT` row or owner was added. The pins that encoded the old behaviour were rewritten in the commit that changed it (the forms' UI tests, `tests/field-primitives.test.ts`, `tests/asset-forms-ui.test.ts`, `tests/api-error-exposure.test.ts`, `tests/api-error-catalog.test.ts`, the sweep tests).
- **Tests:** new files `tests/v-track-s.test.ts` (11), `tests/v-track-f.test.ts` (7), `tests/validation-guards.test.ts` (6), `tests/v-track-m.test.ts` (11) and `tests/v-track-docs.test.ts` (1); deleted `tests/supplier-api-error.test.ts` (4) and `tests/supplier-form-errors.test.ts` (2); 2226 → 2256 tests.
- **Rulings of this plan:** R1 (the earlier rulings), R2 (the server, S-R1–S-R13), R3 (the foundation, F-R1–F-R10), R4 (the forms, M-R1–M-R14), R5 (assembly, A-R1–A-R11).
- **The user's answers to the open issues (2026-10-17):** 1 the category update duplicate stays in (409 under the code); 2 the four remaining duplicates are fixed in Task 29a; 3 "Employee already has a user account" goes under the employee field (Task 29a); 4 the disposal evidence domain errors are decided in the disposal plan; 5 the 17 `common.validation` messages as drafted, read on Task 30's screenshots; 6 a duplicate shows the shared text "ข้อมูลนี้มีอยู่แล้วในระบบ" under its field (also for supplier, username, role name and branch code); 7 Enter saves the report preset name.
- **Not taken:** `react-hook-form`, `aria-invalid` on the SearchableSelect's button (it is a `<button>`), an alert role per field error, a new server rule, a `STAR_ONLY` or `SELECT_STAR_ONLY` change (their selects are in later-plan forms).
- **Handed on:** the later plans of the track, in order: the asset forms (the batch's `rows.<i>` keys, the date pairs, the license seats), the operations forms, the audit forms, the disposal and maintenance forms, the settings form and the prefix editor (`settings.<key>`, `reveal` for the tabs), then the final plan (DESIGN.md's form-error rule, `docs/14_UI_UX_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md`); then the E2E suite and the one deploy (wiki 27).
- Verification: `npm run verify` passes (tests 2,256 · pass 2,255 · skipped 1 · build 60/60 pages).
- Dev-app check: checked (Task 30) on `next start` (port 3110) of the production build of the branch at `0e3124b` (built after deleting `.next/cache/turbopack`) with the dev database, as the seed admin; verify there: lint 0 errors (146 warnings, all under `.agents/`), tests 2255 / 2254 pass / 0 fail / 1 skipped, tsc clean, build 60/60 pages. Nothing was written: every non-GET request was routed in the browser, aborted or fulfilled with a stand-in refusal body, and none reached the server (0 writes). Requests: POST `/api/brands` ×2, POST `/api/suppliers`, POST `/api/admin/integration-clients` and PUT `/api/admin/settings` (data-quality), all intercepted; every other submit sent no request (the client `validate()` stopped it). Counts after: 17 `<form>` tags with `noValidate` in `src` (check-in and the 16 plan-1 forms); 4092 message keys per locale. Empty submit on 11 forms (brand, model, branch, category, company, department, employee, location, supplier, user, role) in light at 1440 and dark at 375: no browser bubble, no toast, no request; focus on the first field of the form's order (`brand-name`, `asset-model-name`, `branch-code`, `category-code`, `company-code`, `department-code`, `employee-code`, `location-code`, `supplier-code`, `admin-user-username`, `role-key`); "กรุณาระบุข้อมูลนี้" under every empty required field; the focused control's description holds its error; no field error has `role="alert"`; the focused field shows inside `main` above the save bar. Bad values: role key "Bad Key" → "รูปแบบไม่ถูกต้อง" and "a" → "ต้องมีอย่างน้อย 2 ตัวอักษร", and typing clears only that field's error; user password "short" → "ต้องมีอย่างน้อย 8 ตัวอักษร", email "x@" → "รูปแบบอีเมลไม่ถูกต้อง", no role → the error under the role group; integration client id "ab" → "ต้องมีอย่างน้อย 3 ตัวอักษร", all scopes unchecked → the error under the scope group (focus on the id first); state review reason "12345" → "ต้องมีอย่างน้อย 10 ตัวอักษร" under the reason, the button enabled, no request; preset name empty → "กรุณาระบุชื่อชุดตัวกรองก่อนบันทึก" under the field. - No migration, database write, schema or dependency change. No server rule was added (spec §6.3): Tasks 20, 27 and 31a only add `fieldErrors` to refusals that existed, and Task 31b changes only how one zod issue reads; status codes and messages are unchanged. Messages (through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`): +9 keys (`asset.duplicateCheckPending`, `asset.batchRowDuplicate`, `auditFinding.closeNeedsEvidence`, `auditFinding.batchNeedsSelection`, `disposalPage.batchNeedsAssets`, `disposalPage.bulkExecutionPermanentConfirmationRequired`, `repairRecord.returnDateBeforeReported`, `common.validation.tooManyDecimals`, `asset.batchRowLimits`) and −8 (`asset.licenseSeatUsageInvalid`, `asset.customFieldRequired`, `asset.duplicateWarning`, `checkout.reviewIncomplete`, `checkin.selectCheckoutRequired`, `checkin.selectReturnAndReceiver`, `checkin.completeRequiredFields`, `transfer.destinationRequired`): 4092 → 4093 keys.
- **The server (Tasks 20, 27, 31a, 31b):** the disposal execution's evidence refusals `DISPOSAL_EVIDENCE_EXCEPTION_REASON_REQUIRED` and `DISPOSAL_EVIDENCE_EXCEPTION_ACK_REQUIRED` keep their `code` and add `fieldErrors` under `evidenceExceptionReason` (`required`, `tooShort` or `tooLong`) and `evidenceExceptionAcknowledged` (`required`) (`getDisposalEvidenceFieldErrors`, `src/lib/disposal-evidence-exception.ts`). A bulk execution preview or commit stays a 200 and adds `fieldErrors` when a blocked row's code points at a field of the dialog (`sharedRecipientName: "recipientRequired"` and the exception's two fields; `getDisposalBulkExecutionFieldErrors`, `src/lib/disposal-bulk-execution.ts`). A repair record's reporter refusals (`reportedById`: `required`, `reporterNotFound`), its vendor refusal on create, complete and edit (`vendorId`: `vendorNotFound`) and the PM plan edit's vendor refusal (`vendorId`: `pmVendorNotFound`) name their field, through the new `fieldErrors` of `MaintenanceApiError` and plan 1's `FieldRefusalError`; before, they reached the person as the generic "เกิดข้อผิดพลาด". Checkin's refusals of the return condition and the return status keep their message and add `fieldErrors` (`conditionAfter: "invalidReturnCondition"`, `nextStatusId: "invalidReturnStatus"`), which the checkin form shows under its selects after closing its review (Task 31a). zod's `.int()` refusing a fraction reads `invalidNumber` instead of `required` (`src/lib/field-errors.ts`, Task 31b).
- **Shared additions (Tasks 8, 21, 28, 31c):** `waitForDialogToClose(frames = 60)` in `src/lib/field-focus.ts` (a form refused while its own review dialog is open closes the dialog before the field takes focus: `reveal: leaveReview`); `step(value, size)` in `src/lib/validation-rules.ts` (a whole-number field's `step` restated, `invalidNumber`) and `decimals(value, places)` beside it (a money field's two decimal places, with its own code `tooManyDecimals` and sentence `common.validation.tooManyDecimals`, Task 31c); `NON_VALIDATION_TOASTS` in `tests/validation-guards.test.ts` (the toasts outside a catch that are not about a field, with an exact count, a reason and a ruling each: the upload results of the disposal request and batch and of the repair record form and dialogs).
- **The forms (Tasks 1–19, 21–26, 29–31, 31c–31e):** the asset form, the batch, the register's bulk update, component install and removal, status correction, the import wizard and label print; checkout, checkin, transfer, bulk move and the transaction cancel dialog; the audit round form, round cancel, mark not found, component missing, the finding dialogs (decision, action plan, close), batch review and the scan check; the disposal request, batch, decision, execution, bulk approval and bulk execution; the PM plan form, the repair record form and its complete, cancel and edit dialogs. Each has `noValidate` straight after `onSubmit`, a module-level `validate()` that restates every constraint its controls carry, errors under the field (a group: one error), focus on the first wrong field, and the server's `fieldErrors` on the same fields; a refusal that names no field keeps today's message. 26 validation toasts went (the asset form's 12, checkout's 4, checkin's 4, the batch's 2, component install, transfer, bulk move and round cancel one each); the batch's duplicate-check result moved into its Notice. Every money field (the asset form's price and document total, the batch's price, the disposal request's, batch's, decision's and execution's values, the repair cost) refuses more than two decimal places with one sentence (Task 31c).
- **Review dialogs and steps (spec §4.2):** checkout, checkin and transfer close their review dialog before the refused field takes focus; a refusal goes back to the batch's edit step, component install's details step, the disposal batch's edit step and bulk execution's review step (also when no row of a preview can run because of the shared values). The audit scan check keeps focus inside its sheet on a phone; offline-queued scans replay as before.
- **Date pairs (spec §7.4, client rules only):** the warranty end not before its start in the asset form (on edit only when one of the two dates changed) and in the batch; the audit round's end not before its start (the pickers limit each other); the label print queue's created-from/to (`min`/`max`); a repair's completion date not before its reported date (`min` and `repairRecord.returnDateBeforeReported`).
- **Gates (spec §4.4):** content gates became errors on submit with the button enabled: status correction's 5 characters, bulk update's location or custodian, the disposal batch's requester and reason, the decision's rejection reason, the execution's historical reason and acknowledgement, bulk execution's shared values and its permanent confirmation. Prerequisites stay gated, `aria-disabled` with a visible reason tied by `aria-describedby`: the asset save on a protected status change or while the duplicate check runs, the import with no ready row, the cancel dialog's blockers, the finding close without evidence, batch review with nothing selected, the scan check's missing photo (pressing the waiting save also opens the note-and-photo section that holds the picker, Task 31e), the disposal batch with fewer than 2 assets, the execution without evidence, the two bulk dialogs with nothing eligible, and the batch's add row at 100 rows, remove row at 2 and check duplicates while the rows hold a duplicate (the row count line names the 2–100 limits, `asset.batchRowLimits`, Task 31d).
- **Spec §5's bugs in these forms:** checkin's return date, bulk move's destination, and the disposal request's asset and requester are checked. The 14 asterisk-only selects show their error (`SELECT_STAR_ONLY` 14 → 0); the license's total seats lost their asterisk (spec §11 call 8).
- **Guards and pins:** `V_FORMS` 15 → 43 files (every `V_SCOPE` file but the settings form, plan 3); FMG `STAR_ONLY` empty; checkin's `required` floor 4 → 5 in `CONSTRAINT_FLOORS`; `NON_VALIDATION_TOASTS` 4 rows; no `EXEMPT`, `V_SCOPE` or `NOT_IN_SCOPE` row added. `<form … noValidate>` in `src` 17 → 46; toasts outside a catch in `src` 61 in 19 files → 34 in 11. The pins that encoded the old behaviour were rewritten in the commit that changed it.
- **Tests:** new files `tests/v-track2-a.test.ts` (7), `tests/v-track2-o.test.ts` (6), `tests/v-track2-u.test.ts` (6), `tests/v-track2-d.test.ts` (7), `tests/v-track2-m.test.ts` (5), `tests/v-track2-x.test.ts` (5, the addendum); one test added to `tests/v-track-docs.test.ts`; `validateHistoricalException` and its test deleted (`src/lib/disposal-bulk-execution-ui.ts`, `tests/disposal-bulk-execution-ui-state.test.ts`); 2256 → 2292 tests.

- **Not taken:** a server rule for the warranty or repair date pairs (spec §11 call 6), `react-hook-form`, a `NO_RULE` allowance for the three forms with nothing to check (an empty `validate()` with a comment, U-R6), `aria-invalid` on a `<button>`, an alert role per field error.

Area U: an end before the start → the error under the end with focus; the pickers limit each other; a stand-in preview 400 `{endDate: endBeforeStart}` → under the end; `{sampleRate: invalidNumber}` → "กรุณากรอกเป็นตัวเลข" (Task 31b), no toast. ## V Track Plan 3 (2026-10-19)

- Branch `feat/v-track-3` in the main checkout (from `master` `ede37dd0`: V track plans 1 and 2 merged and pushed at `f1ae1828`, the asset partial-save fix on top; not deployed). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-v-track-field-errors-design.md` (the user's decisions U1–U9) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-v-track-plan3.md` (plan 3 of the track: the system settings form and its prefix editor, spec §5 #45 and #46).
- No migration, database write, schema, dependency or server change: `src/app/api/admin/settings/route.ts` and `src/lib/validations/system-settings.ts` are as plan 1 left them. Messages (through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`): +1 key (`systemSettingsPage.prefixCategoriesRequired`) and −11 (`systemSettingsPage.duplicateCategory`, `invalidFormatTemplate`, `invalidPublicQrBaseUrl`, `invalidLabelTemplate`, `invalidLabelSize`, `invalidOperationDocumentTemplate`, `invalidNotificationRule`, `invalidRetentionPolicy`, `invalidAccountingDepreciationPolicy`, `invalidWorkflowApproval`, `invalidSchedulerSchedule`): 4093 → 4083 keys.
- **The rules (Task 1):** `src/lib/system-settings-field-rules.ts` holds the settings form's rules setting by setting: `settingRuleCode` answers the code the server's `systemSettingsUpdateSchema` answers for the same setting (its api-error catalog key), `settingControlCode` restates what the controls allowed before `noValidate` (running digits 1–12, the LDAP deactivation limit ≥ 0, whole numbers, a label's margin and gap in steps of 0.5), `settingSaveErrors` and `settingLiveErrors` add the 5000-character limit and the prefix groups; a save checks the controls' own limits only on the settings it changes (open issue 13), so a stored value only they refuse does not stop a save of another setting. `tests/v-track3.test.ts` runs it and the schema on the same values. `getSystemSettingsTabForKey("accounting_depreciation_policy")` now answers the organization tab (it answered "advanced"; the change review's link for that setting opens the right tab too).
- **The form (Tasks 2, 3):** `<form onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate …>`, a module-level `validate()`, `useFieldErrors` keyed `settings.<key>` like the route's `fieldErrors`. Every setting the form shows has its error under its field (`SETTING_FIELD_IDS`, in visual order; the advanced settings as `setting-<key>`); a save goes to the first wrong one, switching to its tab first and opening a closed `<details>` (the advanced table, the depreciation JSON); a refusal of the server that names a setting lands the same way, and any other keeps today's toast. The 12 validation toasts went, and the 12 danger boxes of the page became each setting's error under its field, still shown while typing (`Field liveError`, spec U8); the scheduler's error now sits under the custom schedule on the LDAP sync tab, not on the automation tab. The approval switches, the label name choice (now a `FieldGroup`), each tape's nine label fields and the depreciation JSON (whose closed section opens while the JSON is invalid) can show their error.
- **The prefix editor (Task 4):** its save is no longer disabled: it checks the prefix (required, 2–10 letters A-Z or digits) and the categories (at least one, `systemSettingsPage.prefixCategoriesRequired`) and focuses the first wrong one inside the dialog; the prefix's rule shows while typing once a prefix is typed (it no longer shows on an empty prefix as the editor opens).
- **Gates (Task 5):** the save while nothing changed and the LDAP sync apply before a preview are `aria-disabled` (focusable), tied by `aria-describedby` to the line that says why (`settings-save-status`, `ldap-sync-no-preview`); their handlers return first.
- **Guards and pins:** `V_FORMS` 43 → 44 (= `V_SCOPE`); toasts outside a catch in `src` 35 in 12 files → 23 in 11; `<form … noValidate>` in `src` 46 → 47; no `EXEMPT`, `V_SCOPE`, `NOT_IN_SCOPE`, `NON_VALIDATION_TOASTS` or FMG row added. The pins that encoded the old behaviour were rewritten in the commit that changed it.
- **Tests:** new file `tests/v-track3.test.ts` (7); one test added to `tests/v-track-docs.test.ts`; 2297 → 2305 tests.
- **Rulings of this plan:** ST-R1–ST-R11.
- **The user's answers to the open issues:** 2026-10-19, "ตามที่แนะนำ" (every recommendation): 1–11 as written; 12 done (the asset partial-save fix merged first, `ede37dd0`, and this plan was re-based on it); 13 the controls' own limits are checked only on the settings a save changes, so a stored value only they refuse does not stop a save of another setting (Tasks 1 and 3); 14 handed to the final plan of the track (a `Field` change so a save's error on a live field is read once).

No fixes (no code change).

## V Track Final Plan (2026-10-19)

- Branch `feat/v-track-final` in the main checkout (from `master` `71a9e95c`: V track plans 1 to 3 merged; not deployed). Spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-17-v-track-field-errors-design.md` (the user's decisions U1–U9) · plan `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-v-track-final.md` (the last plan of the track: one whole-number rule, the label print queue's gates, a live field read once, and the form-error rule in the design docs, spec §9 item 4).
- No migration, database write, schema, dependency or server change. Messages (through `scripts/messages-edit.mjs`): +1 key, `common.validation.notWholeNumber` ("กรุณากรอกเป็นจำนวนเต็ม" / "Enter a whole number"): 4083 → 4084 keys.
- **One whole-number rule (Task 1):** `wholeNumber` in `src/lib/validation-rules.ts` (an empty value is left to `required`, text that is not a number is `invalidNumber`, a fraction is `notWholeNumber` and reads `common.validation.notWholeNumber`) replaces plan 2's three restatements (P-R8): the license seats' `pattern(…, /\d+/)`, which read "รูปแบบไม่ถูกต้อง", the audit round's own helper, and `step(v, 1)` in the PM days and in the settings' running digits, deactivation limit and label sizes. The server is unchanged: zod's `.int()` still answers `invalidNumber`.
- **The label print queue (Task 2):** print, print one test label and add filtered are `aria-disabled` while they cannot run, tied by `aria-describedby` to the line that says why (`asset-label-print-reason`, the empty note "ยังไม่ได้เลือกทรัพย์สิน"; `asset-label-add-filtered-reason`, "ไม่มีรายการในขอบเขตนี้" beside the button, whose label now stays "เพิ่มตามตัวกรอง"); their handlers return first (plan 2 open issue 16).
- **A live field is read once (Task 3):** `Field liveError` takes the text of the check that runs while typing (`null` while it finds nothing) instead of a flag, and its polite region holds only that text; an error that a save or the server set shows under the field and is read once, by focus (plan 3 open issue 14). Its callers: the asset form's tag and serial, the batch's four row fields (`rowLiveError`), the 15 live settings (`settingLiveError`), each tape's nine label fields (`liveErrorFor`), the depreciation JSON (`jsonLiveError`) and the prefix editor's prefix.
- **The docs (Task 4):** `DESIGN.md` §5 **Forms: Errors Under the Field** (The Error Under the Field Rule: `noValidate` and `validate()`, errors under the field, focus to the first wrong field with `reveal`, the server's `fieldErrors`, no validation toast, prerequisite gates with a visible reason, one announcement per save), with the overview, the Thai Copy Rule, buttons, dialogs, `SearchableSelect`, toasts, live regions, `Field`, groups and the scan check brought in line, one Do and one Don't; `docs/14_UI_UX_DESIGN_SYSTEM.md` (its rows, a paragraph on the V guards, a Form errors registry row, a QA line); `.impeccable/design.json` (the gate, the field's error line, the dialog, one Do and one Don't; no narrative rule, whose sections are colors, typography and elevation only); the guard and pin comments that still said the V track was to come.

## Open Go-Live Decisions

- Confirm production database user and least-privilege permissions.
- Confirm backup owner, restore approver, RTO, and RPO.
- Confirm Public QR Base URL and Brother printer driver tape size before printing physical labels in volume.
- Confirm LDAP/AD auto-provision default role and Employee matching rules with a real AD login account.
- Confirm LDAP/AD scheduled sync safety threshold before enabling automatic deactivation.
- Confirm notification delivery channels beyond in-app notifications.
- `asset_management` had no backup between 2026-08-31 and the 2026-10-07 COPY_ONLY copy, and no log backups in FULL recovery. Schedule full + log backups and copy them off the server.
- Every application on the shared SQL Server instance uses `sa`. Create a least-privilege login for the Production app before rotating the `sa` password.
- Rotate the AD bind account password (it was exposed through the settings API and System Log before security round 1) and decide whether to purge old `system_logs` rows that contain it.
- Confirm the Production `AUTH_SECRET` is not the placeholder value previously used in developer `.env` files.
- Cancel the leftover Draft audit round `AUD-2026-0002 "Test"`; it inflates Work Center and audit coverage figures, and because disposal readiness blocks assets in any non-closed audit round it also blocks disposal of nearly every asset.
- Apply `prisma/manual-migrations/2026-10-07-add-active-checkout-unique-index.sql` to Production after a verified backup (it stops without changes if an asset already has two active checkouts).
- Apply `prisma/manual-migrations/2026-10-07-add-maintenance-outcome.sql` to Production after a verified backup and explicit approval, before deploying `feat/simple-repair-records` (the new code reads and writes `maintenance_tickets.outcome`).
- Remove any server systemd timer or cron entry that still calls `pm:generate-due`, and drop `MAINTENANCE_PM_GENERATION_TOKEN` from the server environment file; the script and endpoint no longer exist.
- Record the assets that sit in `Pending Repair`/`Under Maintenance` with no repair record (10 in the 2026-10-07 copy of Production; listed under "ทรัพย์สินค้างสถานะซ่อมแต่ไม่มีบันทึก" on the Maintenance page) after go-live.
